    <script src="script.js"></script>
    <script>
        function logout() {
            localStorage.removeItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
            window.location.href = 'index.html';
        }

        function openSiemModal(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const m = document.getElementById('siemModal');
            m.style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').focus();
        }
        function closeSiemModal() {
            document.getElementById('siemModal').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').value = '';
        }
        function goToSiem() {
            const key = document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').value.trim();
            if (!key) {
                document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').style.borderColor = '#ef4444';
                setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').style.borderColor = '#1e2d45', 1500);
                return;
            }
            sessionStorage.setItem('siem_api_key', key);
            window.location.href = 'dashboard.html';
        }
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').addEventListener('keydown', e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') goToSiem();
                if (e.key === 'Escape') closeSiemModal();
            });
            document.getElementById('siemModal').addEventListener('click', e => {
                if (e.target === document.getElementById('siemModal')) closeSiemModal();
            });
        });

        let users = [];

        window.onload = function() {
            const current = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
            if (current !== 'admin') {
                window.location.href = 'index.html';
                return;
            }

            users = getAllUsers();
            populateTable();
            calculateStats();
        };

        function populateTable() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';

            if (users.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; color: var(--gray-500); padding: 40px;">No employees registered yet.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            users.forEach(user => {
                const prog = user.progress || {};
                let modsCompleted = 0;
                let totalScore = 0;
                
                for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
                    if (prog[`module${i}`] && prog[`module${i}`].passed) {
                        modsCompleted++;
                        totalScore += prog[`module${i}`].score;
                    }
                }

                const avgScore = modsCompleted > 0 ? Math.round(totalScore / modsCompleted) : 0;
                
                let statusHtml = '';
                if (modsCompleted === 6) {
                    statusHtml = `<span class="progress-pill-admin pill-success"><i class="ti ti-check" style="margin-right: 4px;"></i> Completed</span>`;
                } else if (modsCompleted > 0) {
                    statusHtml = `<span class="progress-pill-admin pill-warning"><i class="ti ti-loader" style="margin-right: 4px;"></i> In Progress</span>`;
                } else {
                    statusHtml = `<span class="progress-pill-admin pill-neutral"><i class="ti ti-minus" style="margin-right: 4px;"></i> Not Started</span>`;
                }

                const preTestScore = prog.preTest ? prog.preTest.score + '%' : '<span style="color:var(--gray-400);">--</span>';
                const surveyDone = prog.survey ? '<span class="progress-pill-admin pill-success"><i class="ti ti-check" style="margin-right:4px;"></i>Done</span>' : '<span class="progress-pill-admin pill-neutral">Pending</span>';

                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy);">${user.empId}</td>
                    <td>${user.name}</td>
                    <td style="color: var(--gray-500);">${user.division}</td>
                    <td>
                        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
                            <div style="flex: 1; height: 6px; background: var(--gray-200); border-radius: 3px; overflow: hidden;">
                                <div style="height: 100%; width: ${(modsCompleted/6)*100}%; background: var(--blue-mid);"></div>
                            </div>
                            <span style="font-weight: 700; font-size: 13px;">${modsCompleted}/6</span>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                    <td style="font-weight: 700;">${avgScore}%</td>
                    <td style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--blue-mid);">${preTestScore}</td>
                    <td>${surveyDone}</td>
                    <td>${statusHtml}</td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        function calculateStats() {
            document.getElementById('statTotalUsers').innerText = users.length;
            
            let completedCount = 0;
            let inProgressCount = 0;
            let sumAvgScore = 0;
            let scoredUsers = 0;

            users.forEach(user => {
                const prog = user.progress || {};
                let mods = 0;
                let userTotalScore = 0;

                for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
                    if (prog[`module${i}`] && prog[`module${i}`].passed) {
                        mods++;
                        userTotalScore += prog[`module${i}`].score;
                    }
                }

                if (mods === 6) completedCount++;
                else if (mods > 0) inProgressCount++;

                if (mods > 0) {
                    sumAvgScore += (userTotalScore / mods);
                    scoredUsers++;
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('statCompleted').innerText = completedCount;
            document.getElementById('statInProgress').innerText = inProgressCount;
            
            const overallAvg = scoredUsers > 0 ? Math.round(sumAvgScore / scoredUsers) : 0;
            document.getElementById('statAvgScore').innerText = overallAvg + '%';
        }

        function filterTable() {
            const input = document.getElementById("searchInput");
            const filter = input.value.toUpperCase();
            const table = document.getElementById("usersTable");
            const tr = table.getElementsByTagName("tr");

            for (let i = 1; i < tr.length; i++) {
                const tdId = tr[i].getElementsByTagName("td")[0];
                const tdName = tr[i].getElementsByTagName("td")[1];
                if (tdId || tdName) {
                    const txtValueId = tdId.textContent || tdId.innerText;
                    const txtValueName = tdName.textContent || tdName.innerText;
                    if (txtValueId.toUpperCase().indexOf(filter) > -1 || txtValueName.toUpperCase().indexOf(filter) > -1) {
                        tr[i].style.display = "";
                    } else {
                        tr[i].style.display = "none";
                    }
                }       
            }
        }
        
        function exportData() {
            const rows = [['Employee ID', 'Name', 'Division', 'Modules Completed', 'Avg Quiz Score', 'Pre-Test Score', 'Post-Assessment Score', 'Improvement', 'Survey Overall Rating', 'Survey Avg Content Quality', 'Status']];
            users.forEach(user => {
                const prog = user.progress || {};
                let mods = 0, totalScore = 0;
                for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
                    if (prog[`module${i}`] && prog[`module${i}`].passed) { mods++; totalScore += prog[`module${i}`].score; }
                }
                const avgScore = mods > 0 ? Math.round(totalScore / mods) + '%' : '0%';
                const status = mods === 6 ? 'Completed' : mods > 0 ? 'In Progress' : 'Not Started';
                const preTest = prog.preTest ? prog.preTest.score + '%' : 'N/A';
                const postTest = prog.postAssessment ? prog.postAssessment.score + '%' : 'N/A';
                let improvement = 'N/A';
                if (prog.preTest && prog.postAssessment) {
                    improvement = (prog.postAssessment.score - prog.preTest.score) + 'pp';
                }
                let surveyRating = 'N/A';
                let surveyContentQuality = 'N/A';
                if (prog.survey && prog.survey.responses) {
                    surveyRating = prog.survey.responses.overall_rating || 'N/A';
                    const r = prog.survey.responses;
                    if (r.q4 && r.q5 && r.q6 && r.q7) {
                        surveyContentQuality = Math.round((parseInt(r.q4) + parseInt(r.q5) + parseInt(r.q6) + parseInt(r.q7)) / 4 * 20) + '%';
                    }
                }
                rows.push([user.empId, user.name, user.division, mods + '/6', avgScore, preTest, postTest, improvement, surveyRating + '/5', surveyContentQuality, status]);
            });
            const csvContent = rows.map(r => r.map(c => `"${c}"`).join(',')).join('\n');
            const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            a.download = `CyberShieldSL_ResearchData_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`;
            a.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <div class="topbar-logo">
        <i class="ti ti-shield-check" style="color:var(--gold);font-size:22px;"></i>
        Cyber<span>Shield</span> SL

<script src="script.js"></script>
<script>
    const currentUserId = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
    if (!currentUserId || currentUserId === 'admin') window.location.href = 'index.html';

    const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
    const user = users.find(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
    if (!user) window.location.href = 'index.html';

    const prog = user.progress || {};
    const MODULE_NAMES = ['Phishing Awareness', 'Password Security', 'Safe Browsing & Email', 'Data Protection & Privacy', 'Social Engineering'];

    let passed = 0, totalScore = 0, lastDate = null;
    for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
        const m = prog[`module${i}`];
        if (m?.passed) {
            passed++;
            totalScore += m.score;
            const d = new Date(m.completedAt || Date.now());
            if (!lastDate || d > lastDate) lastDate = d;
        }
    }

    const allPassed = passed === 5;

    if (allPassed) {
        document.getElementById('certContent').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('certName').textContent = user.name || 'Employee';
        document.getElementById('certEmpId').textContent = `${user.empId}  •  ${user.division || 'Gampaha Divisional Secretariat'}`;
        document.getElementById('certAvgScore').textContent = Math.round(totalScore / 5) + '%';
        document.getElementById('certDate').textContent = lastDate
            ? lastDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
            : new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
    } else {
        document.getElementById('certLocked').style.display = 'block';
        const list = document.getElementById('certProgressList');
        for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            const done = prog[`module${i}`]?.passed;
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = `cpl-item ${done ? 'done' : 'pending'}`;
            item.innerHTML = `<i class="ti ${done ? 'ti-circle-check' : 'ti-circle'} cpl-icon"></i> Module ${i}: ${MODULE_NAMES[i-1]}`;
            list.appendChild(item);
        }
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

=== FILE: cybershield_db.sql ===

-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- CyberShield SL — MySQL Database Schema
-- Database Name: cybershield_db
-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS `cybershield_db` DEFAULT CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
USE `cybershield_db`;

-- 1. USERS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `users` (
  `id` INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  `emp_id` VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  `first_name` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  `last_name` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  `division` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  `password` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  `role` ENUM('employee', 'admin') DEFAULT 'employee',
  `registered_at` DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;

-- 2. MODULE PROGRESS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `module_progress` (
  `id` INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  `emp_id` VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  `module_num` INT NOT NULL,
  `score` INT DEFAULT 0,
  `passed` TINYINT(1) DEFAULT 0,
  `completed_at` DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  FOREIGN KEY (`emp_id`) REFERENCES `users`(`emp_id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;

-- 3. SIEM SECURITY LOGS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `siem_logs` (
  `id` INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  `event_id` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  `timestamp` DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `event_type` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  `severity` ENUM('CRITICAL', 'HIGH', 'MEDIUM', 'LOW', 'INFO') NOT NULL,
  `source_ip` VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  `user_id` VARCHAR(50),
  `description` TEXT
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4;

-- 4. INSERT DEFAULT DEMO DATA
INSERT INTO `users` (`emp_id`, `first_name`, `last_name`, `division`, `password`, `role`) VALUES
('EMP-ADMIN', 'System', 'Administrator', 'IT & Systems', 'admin123', 'admin'),
('EMP-001', 'Kamal', 'Perera', 'Administration', 'password123', 'employee')
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE `emp_id`=`emp_id`;

  <!-- ── TOAST CONTAINER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
  <div id="toast-container"></div>

  <!-- ── JAVASCRIPT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
  <script>
    // ── ADMIN AUTH GUARD ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    (function () {
      const currentUser = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
      if (currentUser !== 'admin') {
        // Redirect non-admin users away
        window.location.href = currentUser ? 'student-dashboard.html' : 'index.html';
      }
    })();

    // ── CONFIG ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const API_BASE = window.location.origin;
    const API_KEY_KEY = 'siem_api_key';
    const MAX_FEED = 100;   // max cards in live feed DOM

    // ── STATE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let apiKey = sessionStorage.getItem(API_KEY_KEY) || '';
    let feedCount = 0;
    let statsInterval = null;
    let es = null;   // EventSource

    // ── CHART DEFAULTS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    Chart.defaults.color = '#64748b';
    Chart.defaults.borderColor = '#1e2d45';
    Chart.defaults.font.family = 'Inter';
    Chart.defaults.animation.duration = 400;

    const SEV_COLORS = {
      CRITICAL: '#ef4444', HIGH: '#f97316',
      MEDIUM: '#eab308', LOW: '#22c55e', INFO: '#3b82f6'
    };

    // ── DONUT CHART ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const donutCtx = document.getElementById('donutChart').getContext('2d');
    const donutChart = new Chart(donutCtx, {
      type: 'doughnut',
      data: {
        labels: ['Critical', 'High', 'Medium', 'Low', 'Info'],
        datasets: [{
          data: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
          backgroundColor: ['#ef4444', '#f97316', '#eab308', '#22c55e', '#3b82f6'],
          borderColor: '#0d1220', borderWidth: 3, hoverOffset: 6
        }]
      },
      options: {
        cutout: '68%', responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: {
          legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 10, padding: 14, font: { size: 11 } } },
          tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => ` ${ctx.label}: ${ctx.raw}` } }
        }
      }
    });

    // ── LINE CHART ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const lineCtx = document.getElementById('lineChart').getContext('2d');
    const lineChart = new Chart(lineCtx, {
      type: 'line',
      data: {
        labels: [], datasets: [{
          label: 'Events', data: [],
          borderColor: '#3b82f6',
          backgroundColor: 'rgba(59,130,246,.1)',
          fill: true, tension: .4,
          pointRadius: 3, pointBackgroundColor: '#3b82f6',
          borderWidth: 2
        }]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
        scales: {
          x: { grid: { color: '#1e2d45' }, ticks: { font: { size: 10 }, maxRotation: 0 } },
          y: { grid: { color: '#1e2d45' }, beginAtZero: true, ticks: { stepSize: 1, font: { size: 10 } } }
        }
      }
    });

    // ── BAR CHART ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const barCtx = document.getElementById('barChart').getContext('2d');
    const barChart = new Chart(barCtx, {
      type: 'bar',
      data: {
        labels: [], datasets: [{
          label: 'Events',
          data: [],
          backgroundColor: 'rgba(139,92,246,.7)',
          borderColor: '#8b5cf6',
          borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 5,
        }]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        indexAxis: 'y',
        plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
        scales: {
          x: { grid: { color: '#1e2d45' }, beginAtZero: true, ticks: { font: { size: 10 } } },
          y: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { font: { size: 10, family: 'JetBrains Mono' } } }
        }
      }
    });

    // ── API KEY MODAL ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function saveKey() {
      const val = document.getElementById('key-input').value.trim();
      if (!val) return;
      apiKey = val;
      sessionStorage.setItem(API_KEY_KEY, val);
      document.getElementById('key-modal').classList.add('hidden');
      init();
    }
    document.getElementById('key-input').addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') saveKey();
    });

    // ── TOAST ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function showToast(event) {
      const sev = event.severity || 'INFO';
      if (!['CRITICAL', 'HIGH'].includes(sev)) return;
      const container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
      const toast = document.createElement('div');
      toast.className = `toast ${sev.toLowerCase()}`;
      toast.innerHTML = `
    <div class="toast-icon">${sev === 'CRITICAL' ? '🚨' : '⚠️'}</div>
    <div class="toast-body">
      <div class="toast-title">${sev}: ${escHtml(event.event_type || 'Unknown')}</div>
      <div class="toast-msg">
        Source: ${escHtml(event.source || '?')} &nbsp;·&nbsp; IP: ${escHtml(event.source_ip || '?')}<br>
        ${event.message ? escHtml(event.message) : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
    <button class="toast-close" onclick="dismissToast(this.closest('.toast'))">✕</button>`;
      container.appendChild(toast);
      setTimeout(() => dismissToast(toast), 7000);
    }

    function dismissToast(el) {
      if (!el || el.classList.contains('fade-out')) return;
      el.classList.add('fade-out');
      setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 360);
    }

    // ── LIVE FEED ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function escHtml(s) {
      return String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
    }

    function fmtTime(isoStr) {
      try {
        return new Date(isoStr).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour12: false });
      } catch { return '—'; }
    }

    function addFeedCard(event) {
      const list = document.getElementById('feed-list');
      // Remove empty placeholder if present
      const empty = list.querySelector('.feed-empty');
      if (empty) empty.remove();

      const sev = (event.severity || 'INFO').toUpperCase();
      const card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = 'event-card';
      card.innerHTML = `
    <span class="sev-badge sev-${sev}">${sev}</span>
    <div class="event-body">
      <div class="event-type">${escHtml(event.event_type || 'UNKNOWN')}</div>
      <div class="event-meta">${escHtml(event.source || '?')} · ${escHtml(event.source_ip || '?')}</div>
      ${event.message ? `<div class="event-msg">${escHtml(event.message)}</div>` : ''}
    </div>
    <div class="event-time">${fmtTime(event.ingested_at)}</div>`;

      list.insertBefore(card, list.firstChild);
      feedCount++;
      document.getElementById('feed-count').textContent = `${feedCount} event${feedCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;

      // Trim DOM to MAX_FEED entries
      while (list.children.length > MAX_FEED) {
        list.removeChild(list.lastChild);
      }
    }

    // ── UPDATE CHARTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function applyStats(stats) {
      // KPI cards
      animateNum('kpi-total', stats.total_events || 0);
      animateNum('kpi-critical', stats.severity_counts?.CRITICAL || 0);
      animateNum('kpi-high', stats.severity_counts?.HIGH || 0);
      animateNum('kpi-sources', stats.active_sources || 0);
      animateNum('kpi-rate', Math.round(stats.events_per_min || 0));
      document.getElementById('hdr-total').textContent = stats.total_events || 0;

      // Donut
      const sc = stats.severity_counts || {};
      donutChart.data.datasets[0].data = [
        sc.CRITICAL || 0, sc.HIGH || 0, sc.MEDIUM || 0, sc.LOW || 0, sc.INFO || 0
      ];
      donutChart.update();

      // Timeline line
      if (stats.timeline) {
        const entries = Object.entries(stats.timeline);
        lineChart.data.labels = entries.map(([k]) => k);
        lineChart.data.datasets[0].data = entries.map(([, v]) => v);
        lineChart.update();
      }

      // Bar chart (top IPs)
      const ips = stats.top_ips || [];
      barChart.data.labels = ips.map(x => x.ip);
      barChart.data.datasets[0].data = ips.map(x => x.count);
      barChart.update();

      // IP panel
      renderIpPanel(ips);
    }

    function renderIpPanel(ips) {
      const panel = document.getElementById('ip-list');
      if (!ips || !ips.length) {
        panel.innerHTML = '<div class="ip-empty">No IP data yet</div>';
        return;
      }
      const max = ips[0]?.count || 1;
      panel.innerHTML = ips.map((ip, i) => `
    <div class="ip-row">
      <span class="ip-rank">#${i + 1}</span>
      <span class="ip-addr">${escHtml(ip.ip)}</span>
      <div class="ip-bar-wrap"><div class="ip-bar" style="width:${Math.round(ip.count / max * 100)}%"></div></div>
      <span class="ip-count">${ip.count}</span>
    </div>`).join('');
    }

    // ── KPI ANIMATION ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const prevValues = {};
    function animateNum(id, target) {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!el) return;
      const start = prevValues[id] || 0;
      if (start === target) return;
      prevValues[id] = target;
      const duration = 500;
      const startTime = performance.now();
      function step(now) {
        const t = Math.min((now - startTime) / duration, 1);
        const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
        el.textContent = Math.round(start + (target - start) * ease);
        if (t < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }

    // ── FETCH INITIAL STATS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function fetchStats() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/stats`, {
          headers: { 'X-API-Key': apiKey }
        });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        applyStats(data);
      } catch { }
    }

    async function fetchHealth() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/health`);
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        document.getElementById('hdr-clients').textContent = data.sse_clients ?? '—';
      } catch { }
    }

    // ── FETCH RECENT EVENTS (initial load) ─────────────────────────────────────
    async function fetchRecentEvents() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/events?limit=30`, {
          headers: { 'X-API-Key': apiKey }
        });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.events && data.events.length) {
          // Add in reverse so newest ends up on top
          const evts = [...data.events].reverse();
          evts.forEach(addFeedCard);
        }
      } catch { }
    }

    // ── SSE CONNECTION ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function connectSSE() {
      if (es) { es.close(); }
      es = new EventSource(`${API_BASE}/api/stream`);

      es.onopen = () => {
        const badge = document.getElementById('live-badge');
        badge.classList.remove('disconnected');
        document.getElementById('live-label').textContent = 'Live';
      };

      es.onmessage = (e) => {
        try {
          const event = JSON.parse(e.data);
          addFeedCard(event);
          showToast(event);
          // Refresh stats after every event
          fetchStats();
        } catch { }
      };

      es.onerror = () => {
        const badge = document.getElementById('live-badge');
        badge.classList.add('disconnected');
        document.getElementById('live-label').textContent = 'Reconnecting…';
        es.close();
        setTimeout(connectSSE, 3000);
      };
    }

    // ── BOOT ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function init() {
      await fetchStats();
      await fetchRecentEvents();
      connectSSE();
      // Poll health every 10s for client count
      statsInterval = setInterval(fetchHealth, 10000);
      fetchHealth();
    }

    // Show modal or init directly
    if (apiKey) {
      document.getElementById('key-modal').classList.add('hidden');
      init();
    } else {
      // Pre-fill the default dev key hint
      document.getElementById('key-input').placeholder = 'siem-dev-secret-2026';
    }
  </script>
</body>

</html>

=== FILE: htb-style.css ===

/* ==========================================================================
   HACK THE BOX ACADEMY STYLE DESIGN SYSTEM — CYBERSHIELD SL
   ========================================================================== */

@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fira+Code:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&display=swap');

:root {
    --htb-bg-darker: #080d16;
    --htb-bg-main: #0b111e;
    --htb-bg-surface: #111927;
    --htb-bg-card: #151e2e;
    --htb-bg-card-hover: #1c283c;
    --htb-border: #1e2c42;
    --htb-border-glow: #2a3e5c;

    --htb-green: #9fef00;
    --htb-green-dark: #76b300;
    --htb-green-glow: rgba(159, 239, 0, 0.18);

    --htb-cyan: #00b4d8;
    --htb-cyan-glow: rgba(0, 180, 216, 0.15);
    
    --htb-purple: #9d4edd;
    --htb-yellow: #ffb703;

<script>
    // ─── PARTICLES ───
    (function() {
        const field = document.getElementById('particleField');
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            const p = document.createElement('div');
            p.className = 'particle';
            p.style.cssText = `
                left: ${Math.random() * 50}%;
                animation-duration: ${8 + Math.random() * 12}s;
                animation-delay: ${Math.random() * 10}s;
                width: ${1 + Math.random() * 2}px;
                height: ${1 + Math.random() * 2}px;
                opacity: ${0.3 + Math.random() * 0.5};
            `;
            field.appendChild(p);
        }
    })();

    // ─── LIVE EMPLOYEE COUNT ───
    (function() {
        const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
        const count = users.filter(u => u.empId !== 'EMP-ADMIN').length;
        let current = 0;
        const target = count + 47; // base offset to make it look realistic
        const el = document.getElementById('statEmployees');
        const interval = setInterval(() => {
            current = Math.min(current + 3, target);
            el.textContent = current;
            if (current >= target) clearInterval(interval);
        }, 40);
    })();

    // ─── INITIALIZE DEFAULT DEMO USERS ───
    (function initDefaultUsers() {
        let users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
        if (users.length === 0) {
            users.push({
                empId: 'EMP-001',
                name: 'Kamal Perera',
                firstName: 'Kamal',
                division: 'Administration',
                password: 'password123',
                progress: {},
                registeredAt: new Date().toISOString()
            });
            localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldUsers', JSON.stringify(users));
        }
    })();

    // ─── QUICK DEMO FILL & FORGOT HELP ───
    function fillDemoUser(empId, pass) {
        document.getElementById('loginId').value = empId;
        document.getElementById('loginPass').value = pass;
        const errEl = document.getElementById('loginError');
        if (errEl) errEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function showForgotModal(e) {
        if (e) e.preventDefault();
        alert("🔑 Password Reset Helpdesk\n\nIf you forgot your Employee ID or password, please contact the Gampaha Divisional Secretariat IT Helpdesk at Extension #402 or email it-helpdesk@gampaha.gov.lk for assistance.");
    }

    // ─── TABS ───
    function switchTab(tab) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.auth-form').forEach(f => f.classList.remove('active'));
        document.querySelectorAll('.auth-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        document.getElementById('form' + tab.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tab.slice(1)).classList.add('active');
        document.getElementById('tab' + tab.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tab.slice(1)).classList.add('active');
    }

    // ─── PASSWORD STRENGTH ───
    function checkStrength(val) {
        let score = 0;
        if (val.length >= 8) score++;
        if (/[A-Z]/.test(val)) score++;
        if (/[0-9]/.test(val)) score++;
        if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(val)) score++;
        const colors = ['', '#EF4444', '#F59E0B', '#3B82F6', '#10B981'];
        const labels = ['—', 'Weak', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Strong'];
        for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
            document.getElementById('s' + i).style.background = i <= score ? colors[score] : '#E2E8F0';
        }
        document.getElementById('strengthLabel').textContent = labels[score];
        document.getElementById('strengthLabel').style.color = colors[score] || '#94A3B8';
    }

    // ─── LOGIN ───
    function doLogin() {
        const id = document.getElementById('loginId').value.trim();
        const pass = document.getElementById('loginPass').value;
        const errEl = document.getElementById('loginError');
        errEl.style.display = 'none';

        if (!id || !pass) { errEl.textContent = 'Please enter your Employee ID and Password.'; errEl.style.display = 'block'; return; }

        if (id === 'EMP-ADMIN' && pass === 'admin123') {
            localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser', 'admin');
            window.location.href = 'admin.html';
            return;
        }

        let users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
        const user = users.find(u => u.empId === id && u.password === pass);
        if (user) {
            localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser', id);
            window.location.href = 'student-dashboard.html';
        } else {
            errEl.textContent = 'Invalid Employee ID or Password. Please try again.';
            errEl.style.display = 'block';
        }
    }

    // ─── REGISTER ───
    function doRegister() {
        const first = document.getElementById('regFirst').value.trim();
        const last = document.getElementById('regLast').value.trim();
        const id = document.getElementById('regId').value.trim().toUpperCase();
        const division = document.getElementById('regDivision').value;
        const pass = document.getElementById('regPass').value;
        const agreed = document.getElementById('agreeTerms').checked;
        const errEl = document.getElementById('registerError');
        const okEl = document.getElementById('registerSuccess');
        errEl.style.display = 'none'; okEl.style.display = 'none';

        if (!first || !last || !id || !division || !pass) { errEl.textContent = 'Please fill in all required fields.'; errEl.style.display = 'block'; return; }
        if (pass.length < 8) { errEl.textContent = 'Password must be at least 8 characters.'; errEl.style.display = 'block'; return; }
        if (!agreed) { errEl.textContent = 'Please agree to the research study terms.'; errEl.style.display = 'block'; return; }

        let users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
        if (users.find(u => u.empId === id)) { errEl.textContent = 'This Employee ID is already registered.'; errEl.style.display = 'block'; return; }

        const newUser = {
            empId: id, name: first + ' ' + last, firstName: first,
            division, password: pass, progress: {},
            registeredAt: new Date().toISOString()
        };
        users.push(newUser);
        localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldUsers', JSON.stringify(users));
        localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser', id);

        okEl.textContent = `Account created! Redirecting to your dashboard...`;
        okEl.style.display = 'block';
        setTimeout(() => window.location.href = 'student-dashboard.html', 1500);
    }

    // ─── SENTRY ───
    CyberSentry.init('login');
</script>
</body>
</html>

=== FILE: lang.js ===
==============================

/**
 * lang.js — CyberShield SL Bilingual Utility
 * Supports: English (en) | Sinhala (si)
 * Usage: add data-en="English text" data-si="සිංහල text" to any element
 */

const LangUtil = (() => {
    const STORAGE_KEY = 'cyberShieldLang';

    function getCurrentLang() {
        return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || 'en';
    }

    function setLang(lang) {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, lang);
        applyLang(lang);
        updateToggleBtn(lang);
    }

    function applyLang(lang) {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-en]').forEach(el => {
            const text = el.getAttribute('data-' + lang);
            if (text !== null) {
                // If element has children (like icons), only update text nodes
                if (el.children.length === 0) {
                    el.textContent = text;
                } else {
                    // Update last text node or use innerHTML carefully
                    el.setAttribute('data-current', text);
                    // Find text nodes and update
                    Array.from(el.childNodes).forEach(node => {
                        if (node.nodeType === 3 && node.textContent.trim()) {
                            node.textContent = ' ' + text;
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
        });

        // Update placeholder attributes
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-placeholder-en]').forEach(el => {
            const ph = el.getAttribute('data-placeholder-' + lang);
            if (ph) el.placeholder = ph;
        });

        // Update page title if defined
        const titleEl = document.querySelector('[data-title-en]');
        if (titleEl) {
            document.title = titleEl.getAttribute('data-title-' + lang) || document.title;
        }

        // Fire custom event for any page-specific handling
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('langChanged', { detail: { lang } }));
    }

    function updateToggleBtn(lang) {
        const btn = document.getElementById('langToggleBtn');
        if (!btn) return;
        if (lang === 'en') {
            btn.innerHTML = '🌐 <span>EN</span> | සි';
            btn.title = 'Switch to Sinhala';
        } else {
            btn.innerHTML = '🌐 EN | <span>සි</span>';
            btn.title = 'Switch to English';
        }
    }

    function toggle() {
        const current = getCurrentLang();
        setLang(current === 'en' ? 'si' : 'en');
    }

    function init() {
        // Apply current language on page load
        const lang = getCurrentLang();
        // Wait for DOM
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
                applyLang(lang);
                updateToggleBtn(lang);
            });
        } else {
            applyLang(lang);
            updateToggleBtn(lang);
        }
    }

    return { init, toggle, getCurrentLang, setLang };
})();

// Auto-init
LangUtil.init();

<script src="sentry.js"></script>
<script>
    const currentUserId = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
    if (!currentUserId || currentUserId === 'admin') window.location.href = 'index.html';

    let allUsers = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
    let sortMode = 'score';

    function computeStats(user) {
        const prog = user.progress || {};
        let passed = 0, totalScore = 0;
        for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
            if (prog[`module${i}`]?.passed) { passed++; totalScore += prog[`module${i}`].score; }
        }
        return { passed, avg: passed > 0 ? Math.round(totalScore / passed) : 0 };
    }

    function getRankedUsers() {
        return allUsers.map(u => ({ ...u, stats: computeStats(u) }))
            .sort((a, b) => {
                if (sortMode === 'score') return b.stats.avg - a.stats.avg || b.stats.passed - a.stats.passed;
                return b.stats.passed - a.stats.passed || b.stats.avg - a.stats.avg;
            });
    }

    function renderPodium(ranked) {
        const podium = document.getElementById('podium');
        const top3 = ranked.slice(0, 3);
        if (top3.length === 0) {
            podium.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;width:100%;padding:40px;"><i class="ti ti-trophy" style="font-size:48px;opacity:0.2;display:block;margin-bottom:12px;"></i>No rankings yet. Complete modules to appear here!</div>';
            return;
        }
        const order = top3.length >= 3 ? [top3[1], top3[0], top3[2]] : top3.length === 2 ? [top3[1], top3[0]] : [top3[0]];
        const classMap = { 0: 'podium-2nd', 1: 'podium-1st', 2: 'podium-3rd' };
        const heights = { 0: 80, 1: 110, 2: 60 };
        const medals = ['🥈', '🥇', '🥉'];
        const rankNums = { 0: 2, 1: 1, 2: 3 };

        podium.innerHTML = order.map((u, i) => {
            if (!u) return '';
            const cls = classMap[i];
            const rankNum = rankNums[i];
            const initial = (u.name || '?')[0].toUpperCase();
            return `
            <div class="podium-place ${cls}">
                <div class="podium-rank">${rankNum === 1 ? '🏆 1st' : rankNum === 2 ? '🥈 2nd' : '🥉 3rd'}</div>
                <div class="podium-avatar">${initial}</div>
                <div class="podium-name">${u.name || 'Unknown'}</div>
                <div class="podium-division">${u.division || '—'}</div>
                <div class="podium-score">${u.stats.avg}% avg</div>
                <div class="podium-stand" style="height:${heights[i]}px;">${medals[i]}</div>
            </div>`;
        }).join('');
    }

    function renderTable(ranked) {
        const tbody = document.getElementById('lbTableBody');
        const query = document.getElementById('searchLb').value.toLowerCase();
        const filtered = ranked.filter(u => (u.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(query) || (u.division || '').toLowerCase().includes(query));

        document.getElementById('emptyState').style.display = filtered.length === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
        tbody.innerHTML = '';

        filtered.forEach((u, idx) => {
            const rank = ranked.indexOf(u) + 1;
            const isMe = u.empId === currentUserId;
            const prog = u.progress || {};
            const dots = Array.from({length: 6}, (_, i) => `<div class="mod-dot ${prog[`module${i+1}`]?.passed ? 'mod-done' : 'mod-pending'}" title="Module ${i+1}"></div>`).join('');
            const rankClass = rank === 1 ? 'rank-1' : rank === 2 ? 'rank-2' : rank === 3 ? 'rank-3' : 'rank-other';
            const status = u.stats.passed === 6 ? '<span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:700;font-size:12px;"><i class="ti ti-circle-check"></i> Complete</span>' :
                u.stats.passed > 0 ? '<span style="color:var(--blue-light);font-weight:600;font-size:12px;">In Progress</span>' :
                '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:12px;">Not Started</span>';

            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            if (isMe) tr.className = 'current-user';
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td><span class="rank-badge ${rankClass}">${rank}</span></td>
                <td><div class="emp-cell">
                    <div class="emp-av">${(u.name||'?')[0].toUpperCase()}</div>
                    <div>
                        <div class="emp-name">${u.name || 'Unknown'}${isMe ? '<span class="you-tag">You</span>' : ''}</div>
                        <div class="emp-div">${u.empId || ''}</div>
                    </div>
                </div></td>
                <td style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:13px;">${u.division || '—'}</td>
                <td><div class="modules-bar">${dots}<span style="margin-left:6px;font-size:12px;color:var(--text-muted);">${u.stats.passed}/6</span></div></td>
                <td><span class="score-val">${u.stats.avg > 0 ? u.stats.avg + '%' : '—'}</span></td>
                <td>${status}</td>`;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
    }

    function sortBy(mode, btn) {
        sortMode = mode;
        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        const ranked = getRankedUsers();
        renderPodium(ranked);
        renderTable(ranked);
    }

    function filterTable() {
        renderTable(getRankedUsers());
    }

    (function init() {
        const ranked = getRankedUsers();
        renderPodium(ranked);
        renderTable(ranked);
        CyberSentry.init('dashboard');
    })();
</script>
</body>
</html>

                    <!-- VISUAL GUIDE CARD WITH EXPLANATION BOX -->
                    <div class="visual-guide-card">

                            <span class="badge" style="background: rgba(59,130,246,0.2); color: #60a5fa; border: 1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.4); font-size:11px; padding:3px 10px; border-radius:12px;">Step-by-Step Officer Guide</span>

                            <img src="gov_phishing_visual_guide.png" alt="Email Security Analysis: Real Gov vs Fake Phishing" class="visual-guide-img">
                        </div>
                        <div class="visual-explanation-box">
                            <div class="visual-explanation-title">
                                🔍 <span>Visual Breakdown: How to Spot Fake Emails at Your Office Desk</span>

                                    <div class="callout-num red">1</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Fake Sender Address (Spoofed Domain)</h4>
                                        <p>Real government emails always end in official domains like <code>@dist.gov.lk</code>. Fake emails use tricks like <code>government.support@tax-refunds.net</code> or <code>admin@gov-lk-update.com</code> to trick officers.</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">
                                    <div class="callout-num red">2</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Urgent Threat Language</h4>
                                        <p>Phrases like "ACT NOW OR ACCOUNT TERMINATED IN 2 HOURS" create panic. Attackers want you to act fast without checking with your IT officer.</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">
                                    <div class="callout-num amber">3</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Generic Salutations</h4>
                                        <p>Instead of your actual name or official designation, scam emails say "Dear Valued Citizen" or "Dear Sir/Madam".</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">
                                    <div class="callout-num red">4</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Malicious Links & Attachments</h4>
                                        <p>Hovering over the link reveals non-government websites (e.g. <code>fake.gov.portal.com</code>). Never open unexpected <code>.exe</code> or zip files!</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="gov-do-dont-grid">
                                <div class="gov-do-card">
                                    <h4>✅ What Government Staff SHOULD Do</h4>
                                    <ul>
                                        <li>Verify the sender's full email address (check the part after @).</li>
                                        <li>Hover over links to inspect the actual destination URL before clicking.</li>
                                        <li>Report suspicious emails immediately to your divisional IT officer.</li>
                                    </ul>
                                </div>
                                <div class="gov-dont-card">
                                    <h4>❌ What Government Staff Should NEVER Do</h4>
                                    <ul>
                                        <li>Never enter government system passwords on external websites.</li>
                                        <li>Never open unexpected email attachments or macro-enabled files.</li>
                                        <li>Never reply to panic-inducing emails asking for NIC or OTP details.</li>
                                    </ul>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-help"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">What is Phishing?</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <p>Phishing is a deceptive email or message attack where criminals pretend to be official government departments, banks, or colleagues to trick you into giving away passwords, citizen identity numbers, or clicking malicious links.</p>
                            
                            <div class="warning-box">
                                <i class="ti ti-alert-triangle"></i>
                                <div>
                                    <strong>IMPORTANT FOR GOVERNMENT OFFICERS:</strong>
                                    <p>Over 90% of security breaches start with a single clicked link in a phishing email. Staying alert protects government servers and citizen confidentiality!</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-category"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">4 Common Types of Phishing</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="info-grid">
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>1. Mass Email Phishing</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Fake circulars or tax/bonus notices sent to hundreds of officers hoping someone clicks.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>2. Targeted Spear Phishing</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Customized scam emails using your exact name, department name, or project details.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>3. SMS Phishing (Smishing)</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Fake mobile SMS claiming your government bank account or salary payment requires urgent link verification.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>4. Phone Phishing (Vishing)</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Fake phone calls from someone pretending to be from Ministry IT asking for your OTP or password.</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-shield-check"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">Simple Action Checklist</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <p>Follow these 5 simple steps whenever an unexpected email lands in your inbox at work:</p>
                            <div class="tip-chips">
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-eye"></i> 1. Check sender domain (@gov.lk)</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-pointer"></i> 2. Hover over links first</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-phone"></i> 3. Call sender directly if unsure</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-trash"></i> 4. Delete unverified emails</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-report"></i> 5. Inform Divisional IT Officer</span>
                            </div>

                            <button class="btn btn-outline" style="border-color: var(--green); color: var(--green);" onclick="judgeEmail('legitimate')"><i class="ti ti-shield-check"></i> It's Legitimate</button>

    <script>
        // Module 1 Specific Logic

        // --- SIMULATOR LOGIC ---
        const emails = [
            {
                senderName: "IT Support Desk",
                senderEmail: "admin@gov-lk-portal.com", // Fake
                subject: "URGENT: Your Gov Portal Account Will Be Suspended",
                body: "Dear Employee,\n\nWe have detected unusual login activity on your government portal account. To prevent your account from being locked out in the next 24 hours, you must verify your identity immediately.\n\nPlease click the link below to verify:\n<a href='#' style='color:blue;'>http://verify.gov-lk-portal.com/login</a>\n\nThank you,\nIT Support",
                type: "phishing",
                reason: "The sender address 'gov-lk-portal.com' is fake (not a real .gov.lk domain), it uses extreme urgency, and provides a suspicious link."
            },
            {
                senderName: "HR Department",
                senderEmail: "hr@gampaha.gov.lk", // Legit
                subject: "Upcoming Mandatory Training Session",
                body: "Dear Colleagues,\n\nPlease note that there will be a mandatory training session on the new e-office system next Tuesday at 10:00 AM in the main auditorium.\n\nNo registration is required. Please ensure you bring your employee ID badge.\n\nRegards,\nHuman Resources\nGampaha Divisional Secretariat",
                type: "legitimate",
                reason: "The sender address is a legitimate .gov.lk domain, it does not ask for sensitive information or urge immediate clicking of suspicious links."
            },
            {
                senderName: "Finance Division",
                senderEmail: "finance.update2023@gmail.com", // Fake
                subject: "Salary Hold Notification - Action Required",
                body: "Hello,\n\nDue to a processing error, your monthly salary has been placed on hold. Please download the attached Excel document to update your bank account details.\n\n<a href='#' style='color:blue;'>[Attachment: Bank_Details_Form.xlsm]</a>\n\nFailure to do so will result in non-payment this month.\n\nFinance Team",
                type: "phishing",
                reason: "Government finance departments do not use @gmail.com accounts. It uses panic tactics (salary hold) and asks you to download a potentially malicious macro-enabled Excel file (.xlsm)."
            }
        ];

        let currentEmailIndex = 0;
        let simScore = 0;

        function renderEmail() {
            if (currentEmailIndex >= emails.length) {
                document.getElementById('emailContainer').innerHTML = "";
                document.getElementById('simActions').style.display = "none";
                document.getElementById('simComplete').style.display = "block";
                return;
            }

            const email = emails[currentEmailIndex];
            const html = `
                <div style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--gray-200); padding-bottom: 16px; margin-bottom: 16px;">
                    <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); font-size: 16px;">From: ${email.senderName} &lt;<span style="color: var(--gray-600); font-weight: normal;">${email.senderEmail}</span>&gt;</div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--navy); margin-top: 8px;">Subject: ${email.subject}</div>
                </div>
                <div style="white-space: pre-wrap; font-family: var(--font-body); color: var(--gray-800);">${email.body}</div>
                <div id="simFeedback" style="margin-top: 24px; padding: 16px; border-radius: 8px; display: none;"></div>
            `;
            document.getElementById('emailContainer').innerHTML = html;
            document.getElementById('simActions').style.display = "flex";
        }

        function judgeEmail(userChoice) {
            document.getElementById('simActions').style.display = "none";
            const email = emails[currentEmailIndex];
            const feedbackEl = document.getElementById('simFeedback');
            feedbackEl.style.display = "block";

            if (userChoice === email.type) {
                simScore++;
                document.getElementById('simScore').innerText = simScore;
                feedbackEl.style.backgroundColor = "var(--green-soft)";
                feedbackEl.style.border = "1px solid var(--green)";
                feedbackEl.innerHTML = `<strong style="color: var(--green);"><i class="ti ti-check"></i> Correct!</strong> It is ${email.type}.<br><span style="font-size:14px; margin-top:8px; display:block;">${email.reason}</span>`;
            } else {
                feedbackEl.style.backgroundColor = "var(--red-soft)";
                feedbackEl.style.border = "1px solid var(--red)";
                feedbackEl.innerHTML = `<strong style="color: var(--red);"><i class="ti ti-x"></i> Incorrect.</strong> It was actually ${email.type}.<br><span style="font-size:14px; margin-top:8px; display:block;">${email.reason}</span>`;
            }

            setTimeout(() => {
                currentEmailIndex++;
                renderEmail();
            }, 3500);
        }

        // Start sim
        document.getElementById('simActions').style.display = "flex";
        renderEmail();

        // --- QUIZ LOGIC ---
        const quizQuestions = [
            {
                text: "What is the primary goal of a phishing attack?",
                options: [
                    "To speed up computer performance",
                    "To trick individuals into revealing sensitive information",
                    "To install new software updates",
                    "To block spam emails"
                ],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Phishing relies on deception to manipulate users into voluntarily handing over confidential data like passwords or financial information."
            },
            {
                scenario: "You receive an email from 'admin@gampaha.gov-update.com' asking you to reset your e-office password immediately.",
                text: "What is the major red flag in this scenario?",
                options: [
                    "The email asks you to reset your password",
                    "The email domain is not an official .gov.lk domain",
                    "It was sent during working hours",
                    "There are no red flags, it is safe"
                ],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Official government emails in Sri Lanka should come from a verified .gov.lk address. Attackers often use similar-looking domains to trick users."
            },
            {
                text: "What distinguishes 'Spear Phishing' from regular phishing?",
                options: [
                    "It targets a specific individual or organization with personalized details",
                    "It is conducted over SMS",
                    "It does not use deceptive links",
                    "It is much easier to detect"
                ],
                correct: 0,
                explanation: "Spear phishing is highly targeted and often uses information the attacker has researched about you to make the email seem legitimate."
            },
            {
                text: "If you receive a suspicious email in your government inbox, what is the best immediate action?",
                options: [
                    "Forward it to all your colleagues to warn them",
                    "Reply to the sender asking if they are legitimate",
                    "Do not click any links and report it to the IT department",
                    "Click the link but do not enter any information"
                ],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Interacting with the email (clicking links or replying) can confirm your address is active or trigger malware downloads. Always report it to IT."
            },
            {
                text: "Which of the following is known as 'Smishing'?",
                options: [
                    "Phishing via phone calls",
                    "Phishing via USB drives",
                    "Phishing via SMS text messages",
                    "Phishing via social media posts"
                ],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Smishing stands for SMS Phishing. Attackers send texts mimicking banks or institutions, asking you to click a link or call a number."
            },
            {
                text: "Why do phishing emails often create a sense of extreme urgency (e.g., 'Your account will be deleted in 2 hours')?",
                options: [
                    "Because government servers are slow",
                    "To force you to act quickly without thinking logically or verifying",
                    "Because IT policies mandate quick responses",
                    "To improve email delivery times"
                ],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Urgency is a psychological trick. When panicked, people are more likely to make mistakes and click malicious links without verifying the sender."
            },
            {
                scenario: "You hover your mouse over a link in an email that says 'www.bank.lk/login'. A small tooltip pops up showing 'http://192.168.4.5/scam'.",
                text: "What should you do?",
                options: [
                    "Click it because the visible text says bank.lk",
                    "Do not click it; the actual destination does not match the display text",
                    "Copy the tooltip address into a new tab",
                    "Reply to the sender asking for clarification"
                ],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Hovering over a link (without clicking) reveals the true URL. If it doesn't match the display text or looks suspicious, it's a phishing attempt."
            },
            {
                text: "According to Parsons et al. (2017), what is often the weakest link in information security systems?",
                options: [
                    "Outdated antivirus software",
                    "Weak firewalls",
                    "The human factor (employees)",
                    "Slow internet speeds"
                ],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Even the best technical defenses can be bypassed if an employee is manipulated into giving away their password or clicking a malicious link."
            },
            {
                text: "Which of these attachments is most commonly used to deliver malware in a phishing email?",
                options: [
                    "A standard .txt file",
                    "An unexpected .xlsm (Macro-enabled Excel) or .zip file",
                    "A small .png image",
                    "A link to a YouTube video"
                ],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Attackers often hide malware inside macros in Office documents or inside compressed .zip archives."
            },
            {
                text: "You receive an email from your division head urgently requesting you to buy gift cards. What is the safest approach?",
                options: [
                    "Buy the gift cards immediately because it's your boss",
                    "Email them back to confirm",
                    "Call the division head on a known phone number to verify the request",
                    "Ignore it and delete the email permanently"
                ],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "This is a common 'CEO Fraud' or impersonation attack. Always verify unusual requests for money or data using a secondary communication method, like a phone call."
            }
        ];

        let currentQIndex = 0;
        let quizScore = 0;

        const quizProgress = document.getElementById('quizProgress');
        const qCurrent = document.getElementById('qCurrent');
        const qScore = document.getElementById('qScore');
        const qScenario = document.getElementById('qScenario');
        const qText = document.getElementById('qText');
        const qOptions = document.getElementById('qOptions');
        const qExplanation = document.getElementById('qExplanation');
        const qExpText = document.getElementById('qExpText');
        const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnNextQuestion');

        // Init Progress Bar
        for(let i=0; i<quizQuestions.length; i++){
            const step = document.createElement('div');
            step.className = 'quiz-step';
            if(i===0) step.classList.add('active');
            quizProgress.appendChild(step);
        }

        function loadQuestion() {
            const q = quizQuestions[currentQIndex];
            qCurrent.innerText = currentQIndex + 1;
            
            if (q.scenario) {
                qScenario.innerText = q.scenario;
                qScenario.style.display = "block";
            } else {
                qScenario.style.display = "none";
            }
            
            qText.innerText = q.text;
            qOptions.innerHTML = "";
            qExplanation.classList.remove('show');

            const letters = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'];
            q.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
                const optEl = document.createElement('div');
                optEl.className = 'quiz-option';
                optEl.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="quiz-option-letter">${letters[idx]}</div>
                    <div class="quiz-option-text">${opt}</div>
                `;
                optEl.onclick = () => handleAnswer(idx);
                qOptions.appendChild(optEl);
            });

            // Update step UI
            const steps = document.querySelectorAll('.quiz-step');
            steps.forEach((s, i) => {
                s.classList.remove('active');
                if(i < currentQIndex) s.classList.add('completed');
                if(i === currentQIndex) s.classList.add('active');
            });
        }

        function handleAnswer(selectedIndex) {
            // Prevent multiple clicks
            if (qExplanation.classList.contains('show')) return;

            const q = quizQuestions[currentQIndex];
            const opts = qOptions.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');

            opts.forEach(o => o.classList.add('disabled'));
            opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('selected');

            if (selectedIndex === q.correct) {
                opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('correct');
                quizScore++;
                qScore.innerText = quizScore;
                document.getElementById('sidebarScore').innerText = quizScore + "/10";
            } else {
                opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('wrong');
                opts[q.correct].classList.add('correct'); // reveal correct
            }

            qExpText.innerText = q.explanation;
            qExplanation.classList.add('show');
        }

        btnNext.onclick = () => {
            currentQIndex++;
            if (currentQIndex < quizQuestions.length) {
                loadQuestion();
            } else {
                showResults();
            }
        };

        function showResults() {
            document.getElementById('quizContainer').style.display = "none";
            const resContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
            resContainer.style.display = "block";

            const percent = (quizScore / quizQuestions.length) * 100;
            const ring = document.getElementById('finalScoreRing');
            const title = document.getElementById('finalResultTitle');
            const badge = document.getElementById('badgeContainer');
            
            ring.innerText = percent + "%";
            document.getElementById('statCorrect').innerText = quizScore;
            document.getElementById('statWrong').innerText = quizQuestions.length - quizScore;
            document.getElementById('sidebarScore').innerText = quizScore + "/10";
            document.getElementById('globalProgress').style.width = "100%";
            document.querySelector('.progress-pill span').innerText = "Progress: 100%";

            // Update Sidebar completion
            const navs = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item');
            navs.forEach(n => n.classList.add('completed'));

            if (percent >= 70) {
                ring.className = "score-ring pass";
                title.innerText = "Congratulations! You Passed.";
                badge.style.display = "inline-block";
                // Save progress
                if (typeof saveModuleProgress === 'function') {
                    saveModuleProgress(1, percent, true);
                }
            } else {
                ring.className = "score-ring fail";
                title.innerText = "Assessment Failed";
                title.style.color = "var(--amber)";
                badge.style.display = "none";
                if (typeof saveModuleProgress === 'function') {
                    saveModuleProgress(1, percent, false);
                }
            }
        }

        // Start Quiz
        loadQuestion();
        CyberSentry.init('module');

    </script>
    <script>
        function switchVideo(lang) {
            document.getElementById('videoEn').style.display = lang === 'en' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('videoSi').style.display = lang === 'si' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('tabEn').classList.toggle('active', lang === 'en');
            document.getElementById('tabSi').classList.toggle('active', lang === 'si');
        }
        // Auto-sync video tab with language toggle
        document.addEventListener('langChanged', (e) => switchVideo(e.detail.lang));
    </script>
    <script src="lang.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
