                            <div class="visual-explanation-title">
                                🎭 <span>Visual Breakdown: How Attackers Manipulate Government Staff</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="visual-callout-grid">
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">
                                    <div class="callout-num red">1</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Phone Call Impersonation (Vishing / Pretexting)</h4>
                                        <p>An attacker calls pretending to be a senior Ministry officer or IT technician demanding your OTP or password. Always hang up and call back via official landline numbers!</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">

                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Physical Tailgating at Office Entrances</h4>
                                        <p>Unidentified visitors slipping through secure office doors behind staff holding tea cups or documents. Always require visitors to sign in and wear official visitor badges.</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">
                                    <div class="callout-num red">3</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Baiting USB Drives ("Secret Salary List")</h4>
                                        <p>Attackers leave USB pen drives labeled with tempting titles like "Staff Bonuses 2026" or "Transfers" in office corridors. Plugging it in installs silent spyware instantly.</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="visual-callout-item">
                                    <div class="callout-num amber">4</div>
                                    <div class="callout-text">
                                        <h4>Urgent Boss / Authority Scams</h4>
                                        <p>Scammers sending WhatsApp messages pretending to be your Divisional Secretary asking you to buy voucher codes or transfer funds urgently. Verify face-to-face first!</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="gov-do-dont-grid">
                                <div class="gov-do-card">
                                    <h4>✅ What Government Staff SHOULD Do</h4>
                                    <ul>
                                        <li>Independently verify callers using official internal extension directories.</li>
                                        <li>Challenge unfamiliar visitors walking in secure office hallways without badges.</li>
                                        <li>Hand any lost USB drives directly to your IT administrator without plugging them in.</li>
                                    </ul>
                                </div>
                                <div class="gov-dont-card">
                                    <h4>❌ What Government Staff Should NEVER Do</h4>
                                    <ul>
                                        <li>Never disclose internal passwords, NIC details, or OTPs over phone calls or WhatsApp.</li>
                                        <li>Never hold open secure server room doors for unverified visitors.</li>
                                        <li>Never act on sudden financial/money transfer requests sent via messaging apps.</li>
                                    </ul>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-users"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">What is Social Engineering?</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <p>Social engineering is the art of manipulating people into performing actions or divulging confidential information. Unlike hacking, it targets human psychology rather than software. Government employees are prime targets because they have access to sensitive citizen data and public resources.</p>
                            <div class="warning-box" style="margin-top: 16px;">
                                <i class="ti ti-alert-triangle"></i>
                                <div>
                                    <strong>94% of Cyberattacks Start with Human Error</strong>
                                    <p>Most successful breaches don't involve cracking encryption — they involve tricking a person. Social engineering is the entry point for the majority of corporate and government data breaches worldwide.</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-theater"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">Common Social Engineering Techniques</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="info-grid">
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>Pretexting</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">The attacker creates a fabricated scenario (a "pretext") to extract information. E.g., "I'm from the IT team and need your password to fix an urgent issue."</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>Impersonation</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Pretending to be a trusted person — a colleague, IT support, a vendor, or a government auditor — to gain access or information.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>Baiting</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Leaving an infected USB drive or offering a "free download" to lure victims into infecting their own systems out of curiosity or greed.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>Tailgating / Piggybacking</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Physically following an authorized person into a restricted area (e.g., a server room) without their own access card.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>Quid Pro Quo</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">Offering a service (e.g., "free IT support") in exchange for information or access credentials.</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="info-item">
                                    <h4>Vishing (Voice Phishing)</h4>
                                    <p class="module-card-desc">A phone call from a fake "bank" or "government authority" demanding urgent action or personal verification.</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-brain"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">Psychological Triggers Used by Attackers</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <ul class="red-flag-list">
                                <li class="red-flag-item"><i class="ti ti-x"></i> <strong>Authority:</strong> "This is the Director-General's office. You must act immediately."</li>
                                <li class="red-flag-item"><i class="ti ti-x"></i> <strong>Urgency:</strong> "If you don't do this in the next 30 minutes, the system will shut down."</li>
                                <li class="red-flag-item"><i class="ti ti-x"></i> <strong>Scarcity/Fear:</strong> "Your account will be permanently deleted unless you verify now."</li>
                                <li class="red-flag-item"><i class="ti ti-x"></i> <strong>Reciprocity:</strong> "I helped you with that report last month, so you can just give me temporary access."</li>
                                <li class="red-flag-item"><i class="ti ti-x"></i> <strong>Social Proof:</strong> "Everyone else in your department has already done this."</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <i class="ti ti-shield-check"></i>
                            <div class="card-title">Building Your Human Firewall</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="tip-chips">
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-question-mark"></i> Always verify identity independently</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-pause"></i> Pause before acting under pressure</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-lock-access"></i> Never share credentials with anyone</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-door"></i> Challenge tailgaters politely</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-report"></i> Report suspicious behavior to IT</span>
                                <span class="tip-chip"><i class="ti ti-phone-call"></i> Call back on verified numbers</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="text-center" style="margin-top: 32px;">
                                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="document.querySelector('[data-target=section3]').click()">Try the Social Engineering Simulator <i class="ti ti-arrow-right"></i></button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

    <script>
        // --- SCENARIO SIMULATOR ---
        let simScore = 0;
        let answeredCount = 0;
        const totalScenarios = 4;

        const feedbacks = {
            1: {
                correct: "Correct! Legitimate IT staff will NEVER ask for your password. This is a Pretexting / Vishing attack. Always call IT back on the official number from the intranet, never the number the caller provides.",
                wrong: "Incorrect. This is a classic social engineering trick. IT departments have administrative access to systems — they never need your password. Your password is yours alone."
            },
            2: {
                correct: "Correct! This is a classic Tailgating attack. Physical security is as important as digital security. Every person in a restricted area must use their own credentials — no exceptions, even if it feels rude.",
                wrong: "Incorrect. Holding the door for someone without an access badge is called 'tailgating'. You could be letting an unauthorized person into a sensitive government area."
            },
            3: {
                correct: "Correct! This is 'CEO Fraud' or 'Business Email Compromise'. Attackers spoof executive email accounts to trick employees into urgent, secretive financial actions. Always verify via a secondary channel (a phone call).",
                wrong: "Incorrect. Replying to a spoofed email reaches the attacker. The correct response is to call the director on a known, verified phone number to confirm the request in person."
            },
            4: {
                correct: "Correct! This is a Baiting attack. Antivirus software cannot always detect zero-day malware on a USB drive. The fact that colleagues have used it makes it worse — the device may already have spread malware. Report to IT immediately.",
                wrong: "Incorrect. Even a quick antivirus scan may not catch sophisticated malware. Baiting attacks work because they exploit curiosity. Never use unknown USB drives on government equipment."
            }
        };

        function answerScenario(num, isCorrect, btn) {
            const opts = document.querySelectorAll(`#opts${num} .response-btn`);
            const feedback = document.getElementById(`fb${num}`);
            
            opts.forEach(b => { b.disabled = true; });
            
            if (isCorrect) {
                btn.classList.add('correct');
                simScore++;
                document.getElementById('simScore').innerText = simScore;
                feedback.className = 'scenario-feedback correct show';
                feedback.innerHTML = `<strong><i class="ti ti-check"></i> Correct!</strong> ${feedbacks[num].correct}`;
            } else {
                btn.classList.add('wrong');
                feedback.className = 'scenario-feedback wrong show';
                feedback.innerHTML = `<strong><i class="ti ti-x"></i> Incorrect.</strong> ${feedbacks[num].wrong}`;
                // Highlight correct
                opts.forEach(b => { if (b.onclick.toString().includes('true')) b.classList.add('correct'); });
            }
            answeredCount++;
            if (answeredCount === totalScenarios) {
                document.getElementById('simComplete').style.display = 'block';
            }
        }

        // --- QUIZ LOGIC ---
        const quizQuestions = [
            {
                text: "Social engineering attacks primarily exploit which vulnerability?",
                options: ["Outdated software patches", "Weak firewall configurations", "Human psychology — trust, authority, fear, and urgency", "Slow internet connections"],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Unlike technical hacking, social engineering doesn't need to find a flaw in a system. It manipulates the most complex part of any security system: the human being."
            },
            {
                text: "What is 'Pretexting'?",
                options: ["Sending a message before the actual email", "Creating a fabricated scenario or false identity to extract information from a target", "Writing a news article before it is verified", "Backing up data before a server update"],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Pretexting involves the attacker inventing a plausible story (a 'pretext') to justify their request. For example, impersonating an IT auditor needing access to a server."
            },
            {
                scenario: "Someone calls your extension saying: 'This is the Ministry of Finance compliance auditor. I need your Employee ID and your system login to complete an urgent audit report. Failure to comply could result in a penalty.'",
                text: "What psychological triggers are being used?",
                options: ["Curiosity and Scarcity", "Authority and Fear (with added Urgency)", "Reciprocity and Social Proof", "None — this sounds like a legitimate request"],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "The attacker is using Authority (Ministry of Finance auditor) and Fear/Urgency (penalty for non-compliance). Legitimate auditors follow official channels and do not cold-call for passwords."
            },
            {
                text: "What is 'Tailgating' in the context of physical security?",
                options: ["Following a vehicle too closely on the road", "Sending too many emails to one person", "Gaining unauthorized physical access to a restricted area by following an authorized person", "A type of phishing attack targeting mobile phones"],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Tailgating is a physical social engineering attack. An unauthorized person waits near a secure door and slips in behind someone who has legitimate access, bypassing all digital security."
            },
            {
                text: "A 'Quid Pro Quo' attack in social engineering involves:",
                options: ["Threatening someone until they comply", "Offering a benefit or service in exchange for information or access", "Leaving infected USB drives in public places", "Creating fake websites"],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Quid pro quo (Latin for 'something for something') attacks offer a service (like fake IT support) in exchange for credentials. The victim feels they are receiving help while being exploited."
            },
            {
                text: "Which of the following is the BEST defense against social engineering attacks?",
                options: ["A stronger antivirus program", "Higher walls and fences around government buildings", "Regular security awareness training and a culture of healthy skepticism", "Blocking all phone calls to the office"],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Technology helps, but since social engineering attacks people, the primary defense must also target people. Ongoing training, awareness, and a culture where it's safe to question or verify unusual requests is the most effective countermeasure."
            },
            {
                scenario: "You receive a WhatsApp message from someone claiming to be a colleague: 'Hey, I'm stuck in a meeting. Can you log into the shared drive and send me the Q3 citizen data file? It's urgent.'",
                text: "What is the safest response?",
                options: ["Send the file immediately because they said it's urgent", "Ask them to confirm their employee ID via WhatsApp before sending", "Ignore the WhatsApp request entirely; call or meet the colleague in person to verify before accessing any shared data", "Forward the message to your supervisor"],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "WhatsApp accounts can be compromised or spoofed. The urgency is a manipulation trigger. Never take data-sharing actions based on unverified messaging app requests alone. Verify with the person directly."
            },
            {
                text: "An attacker leaves a USB drive labeled 'Staff Salary Adjustments 2025' in the common area. What type of social engineering attack is this?",
                options: ["Vishing", "Baiting", "Pretexting", "Tailgating"],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "This is a Baiting attack. The attacker labels the USB with something enticing and emotionally triggering (like salary information) to ensure a curious employee will plug it in, thereby infecting the system with malware."
            },
            {
                text: "If you are ever asked to break a security policy for a seemingly good reason, what should you do?",
                options: ["Break the policy if the reason sounds genuine enough", "Always follow the established security policy, regardless of the reason given, and escalate the request to your supervisor", "Follow your gut feeling and decide case by case", "Ask your colleague next to you for advice"],
                correct: 1,
                explanation: "Security policies exist for good reasons. If someone needs an exception, there is a formal process for that. Social engineers deliberately create situations where breaking rules seems justified. Never deviate from policy without authorization from a supervisor."
            },
            {
                text: "What does 'Vishing' mean?",
                options: ["Visual phishing using images", "Video phishing through streaming platforms", "Voice phishing — using phone calls to socially engineer victims", "Phishing using VPN tunnels"],
                correct: 2,
                explanation: "Vishing uses phone calls to deceive targets. Attackers often impersonate banks, government agencies, or IT departments. It is particularly effective because a human voice creates a sense of immediacy and authority."
            }
        ];

        let currentQIndex = 0;
        let quizScore = 0;
        const quizProgress = document.getElementById('quizProgress');
        const qCurrent = document.getElementById('qCurrent');
        const qScore = document.getElementById('qScore');
        const qScenario = document.getElementById('qScenario');
        const qText = document.getElementById('qText');
        const qOptions = document.getElementById('qOptions');
        const qExplanation = document.getElementById('qExplanation');
        const qExpText = document.getElementById('qExpText');
        const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnNextQuestion');

        for (let i = 0; i < quizQuestions.length; i++) {
            const step = document.createElement('div');
            step.className = 'quiz-step';
            if (i === 0) step.classList.add('active');
            quizProgress.appendChild(step);
        }

        function loadQuestion() {
            const q = quizQuestions[currentQIndex];
            qCurrent.innerText = currentQIndex + 1;
            if (q.scenario) { qScenario.innerText = q.scenario; qScenario.style.display = "block"; }
            else { qScenario.style.display = "none"; }
            qText.innerText = q.text;
            qOptions.innerHTML = "";
            qExplanation.classList.remove('show');
            const letters = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'];
            q.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
                const optEl = document.createElement('div');
                optEl.className = 'quiz-option';
                optEl.innerHTML = `<div class="quiz-option-letter">${letters[idx]}</div><div class="quiz-option-text">${opt}</div>`;
                optEl.onclick = () => handleAnswer(idx);
                qOptions.appendChild(optEl);
            });
            const steps = document.querySelectorAll('.quiz-step');
            steps.forEach((s, i) => {
                s.classList.remove('active');
                if (i < currentQIndex) s.classList.add('completed');
                if (i === currentQIndex) s.classList.add('active');
            });
        }

        function handleAnswer(selectedIndex) {
            if (qExplanation.classList.contains('show')) return;
            const q = quizQuestions[currentQIndex];
            const opts = qOptions.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');
            opts.forEach(o => o.classList.add('disabled'));
            opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('selected');
            if (selectedIndex === q.correct) {
                opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('correct');
                quizScore++;
                qScore.innerText = quizScore;
                document.getElementById('sidebarScore').innerText = quizScore + "/10";
            } else {
                opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('wrong');
                opts[q.correct].classList.add('correct');
            }
            qExpText.innerText = q.explanation;
            qExplanation.classList.add('show');
        }

        btnNext.onclick = () => {
            currentQIndex++;
            if (currentQIndex < quizQuestions.length) { loadQuestion(); }
            else { showResults(); }
        };

        function showResults() {
            document.getElementById('quizContainer').style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById('resultContainer').style.display = "block";
            const percent = (quizScore / quizQuestions.length) * 100;
            const ring = document.getElementById('finalScoreRing');
            const title = document.getElementById('finalResultTitle');
            const badge = document.getElementById('badgeContainer');
            ring.innerText = percent + "%";
            document.getElementById('statCorrect').innerText = quizScore;
            document.getElementById('statWrong').innerText = quizQuestions.length - quizScore;
            document.getElementById('sidebarScore').innerText = quizScore + "/10";
            document.getElementById('globalProgress').style.width = "100%";
            document.querySelector('.progress-pill span').innerText = "Progress: 100%";
            document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(n => n.classList.add('completed'));
            if (percent >= 70) {
                ring.className = "score-ring pass";
                title.innerText = "Congratulations! You Passed.";
                badge.style.display = "inline-block";
                if (typeof saveModuleProgress === 'function') saveModuleProgress(5, percent, true);
            } else {
                ring.className = "score-ring fail";
                title.innerText = "Assessment Failed. Please try again.";
                title.style.color = "var(--amber)";
                badge.style.display = "none";
                if (typeof saveModuleProgress === 'function') saveModuleProgress(5, percent, false);
            }
        }

        loadQuestion();
        CyberSentry.init('module');
    </script>
    <script>
        function switchVideo(lang) {
            document.getElementById('videoEn').style.display = lang === 'en' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('videoSi').style.display = lang === 'si' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('tabEn').classList.toggle('active', lang === 'en');
            document.getElementById('tabSi').classList.toggle('active', lang === 'si');
        }
        document.addEventListener('langChanged', (e) => switchVideo(e.detail.lang));
    </script>
    <script src="lang.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    // Same 20 questions as pre-assessment (re-test format)
    const questions = [
        { topic: 'Phishing', text: "What is the primary goal of a phishing attack?", options: ["Speed up computer performance", "Trick users into revealing sensitive information", "Install legitimate software updates", "Block unwanted spam"], correct: 1, explanation: "Phishing uses deception to manipulate users into providing passwords, financial information, or other sensitive data." },
        { topic: 'Phishing', text: "Which is a red flag indicating a phishing email?", options: ["It is addressed to you by name from a known colleague", "It comes from an official .gov.lk address", "It creates extreme urgency ('Your account will be deleted in 1 hour!')", "It contains no links or attachments"], correct: 2, explanation: "Artificial urgency is a classic phishing tactic designed to bypass rational thinking." },
        { topic: 'Phishing', text: "What is 'Smishing'?", options: ["Phishing via phone calls", "Phishing via SMS text messages", "A type of computer virus", "A social media scam"], correct: 1, explanation: "Smishing uses SMS messages to deliver phishing attacks, often mimicking banks or government agencies." },
        { topic: 'Phishing', scenario: "You receive an email from 'accounts@gampaha-secretariat.org' (not .gov.lk) asking you to verify your login.", text: "What is the MOST appropriate response?", options: ["Verify by clicking the link", "Email the sender to confirm", "Do not click any links; report to IT using the official IT contact", "Forward to colleagues to warn them from that computer"], correct: 2, explanation: "The domain '.org' is not the official '.gov.lk' domain and is likely a phishing attempt." },
        { topic: 'Password Security', text: "Which is considered the strongest password?", options: ["pass123", "Admin@2024", "BlueSkyMango!River42", "Gampaha#1"], correct: 2, explanation: "Length is the most critical factor in password strength. A long passphrase is exponentially harder to crack." },
        { topic: 'Password Security', text: "What does Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) provide?", options: ["Faster login speeds", "An extra layer of security requiring a second form of verification", "Automatic password generation", "Encrypted file storage"], correct: 1, explanation: "MFA requires both something you know (password) and something you have (phone/token), making compromise much harder." },
        { topic: 'Password Security', text: "Why should you NOT reuse your personal email password for government systems?", options: ["Personal emails have different character limits", "If your personal account is compromised, attackers can use the same credentials to access government systems", "Government passwords must be shorter", "There is no reason — reusing strong passwords is acceptable"], correct: 1, explanation: "Credential stuffing attacks use stolen passwords from one breach to try logging into other services." },
        { topic: 'Password Security', text: "A colleague asks to use your account temporarily. What should you do?", options: ["Share it since it's a trusted colleague", "Share it but change it the next day", "Refuse and advise them to contact IT for a password reset", "Write your password on a sticky note for them"], correct: 2, explanation: "Sharing login credentials violates government IT policy and creates personal liability." },
        { topic: 'Malware', text: "What type of malware encrypts files and demands payment for the decryption key?", options: ["Spyware", "Adware", "Ransomware", "Worm"], correct: 2, explanation: "Ransomware has become one of the most damaging threats to government institutions." },
        { topic: 'Malware', text: "You find a USB drive labeled 'Ministry Budget Files' in the car park. What should you do?", options: ["Plug it in to see if it belongs to a colleague", "Plug it in but run a virus scan first", "Hand it to IT without plugging it in anywhere", "Put it in the lost and found box"], correct: 2, explanation: "This is a Baiting attack. Only IT with proper tools should handle unknown drives." },
        { topic: 'Malware', text: "Which action BEST prevents malware infections on government workstations?", options: ["Restarting your computer daily", "Keeping the operating system and software fully updated with security patches", "Changing your wallpaper regularly", "Using a personal email for work files"], correct: 1, explanation: "Software patches fix security vulnerabilities that malware actively exploits." },
        { topic: 'Malware', text: "If you suspect your workstation has malware, what is the FIRST action you should take?", options: ["Try to delete the suspected files yourself", "Disconnect from the network and contact IT immediately", "Continue working and see if it resolves itself", "Email your supervisor from the infected machine"], correct: 1, explanation: "Isolating an infected machine prevents the malware from spreading to other government computers." },
        { topic: 'Safe Internet', text: "What does the padlock icon 🔒 and 'https://' in a website address indicate?", options: ["The website is fast", "The connection is encrypted and your data is protected", "The website is government-owned", "The website has been verified by antivirus"], correct: 1, explanation: "HTTPS encrypts all data transmitted between you and the website." },
        { topic: 'Safe Internet', text: "What is the main danger of using public Wi-Fi for government work?", options: ["The Wi-Fi speed is too slow", "Attackers can intercept unencrypted data sent over the network", "Public Wi-Fi doesn't work on government laptops", "It costs more data"], correct: 1, explanation: "Public Wi-Fi is unencrypted. A Man-in-the-Middle attack allows an attacker to capture your login credentials." },
        { topic: 'Safe Internet', text: "After using a government portal on a shared office computer, what MUST you do?", options: ["Just close the browser window", "Properly log out and clear the browser session/cookies", "Turn off the monitor", "Nothing — the session expires automatically"], correct: 1, explanation: "Closing the browser doesn't always end the session. Always log out explicitly on shared computers." },
        { topic: 'Social Engineering', text: "Someone claims to be an IT auditor and needs your system password to conduct a compliance audit. This is:", options: ["A legitimate audit procedure", "A Pretexting social engineering attack", "A Malware infection", "A technical support request"], correct: 1, explanation: "Legitimate IT auditors and support teams never need your password." },
        { topic: 'Social Engineering', text: "An unknown person in professional attire follows you through a security door. This is called:", options: ["Vishing", "Spear phishing", "Tailgating/Piggybacking", "Shoulder surfing"], correct: 2, explanation: "Tailgating bypasses physical access controls by piggybacking on an authorized person's entry." },
        { topic: 'Social Engineering', text: "Which psychological tactic do social engineers MOST frequently use?", options: ["Offering compliments", "Creating urgency and invoking authority figures", "Sending large file attachments", "Using technical jargon"], correct: 1, explanation: "Creating urgency combined with authority bypasses rational decision-making." },
        { topic: 'Data Protection', text: "Under Sri Lanka's PDPA 2022, what does 'Data Minimisation' require?", options: ["Minimise the physical size of data files", "Only collect and process personal data strictly necessary for the stated purpose", "Delete all personal data every month", "Share data only via minimised email attachments"], correct: 1, explanation: "Data Minimisation means collect only what you need, for the specific purpose you need it for." },
        { topic: 'Data Protection', text: "A confidential citizen welfare document is no longer needed. How should it be disposed of?", options: ["Put in the general recycling bin", "Tear in half and discard", "Shred using a cross-cut shredder and dispose of securely", "Store in a drawer indefinitely"], correct: 2, explanation: "Confidential documents must be shredded to ensure the information cannot be reconstructed." }
    ];

    const currentUserId = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
    if (!currentUserId || currentUserId === 'admin') window.location.href = 'index.html';
    const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
    const user = users.find(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
    const preTestScore = user?.progress?.preTest?.score;
    const preTopicScores = user?.progress?.preTest?.topicScores || {};

    // Show pre score hint
    if (preTestScore !== undefined) {
        document.getElementById('preScoreHint').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('preScoreDisplay').textContent = preTestScore + '%';
    }

    let currentQIndex = 0, score = 0;
    const topicScores = {};
    questions.forEach(q => {
        if (!topicScores[q.topic]) topicScores[q.topic] = { correct: 0, total: 0 };
        topicScores[q.topic].total++;
    });

    const quizProgress = document.getElementById('quizProgress');
    const qCurrent = document.getElementById('qCurrent');
    const qScenario = document.getElementById('qScenario');
    const qText = document.getElementById('qText');
    const qOptions = document.getElementById('qOptions');
    const qExplanation = document.getElementById('qExplanation');
    const qExpText = document.getElementById('qExpText');
    const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnNextQuestion');

    for (let i = 0; i < questions.length; i++) {
        const step = document.createElement('div');
        step.className = 'quiz-step' + (i === 0 ? ' active' : '');
        quizProgress.appendChild(step);
    }

    function loadQuestion() {
        const q = questions[currentQIndex];
        qCurrent.innerText = currentQIndex + 1;
        qScenario.style.display = q.scenario ? 'block' : 'none';
        if (q.scenario) qScenario.innerText = q.scenario;
        qText.innerText = q.text;
        qOptions.innerHTML = '';
        qExplanation.classList.remove('show');
        const letters = ['A','B','C','D'];
        q.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = 'quiz-option';
            el.innerHTML = `<div class="quiz-option-letter">${letters[idx]}</div><div class="quiz-option-text">${opt}</div>`;
            el.onclick = () => handleAnswer(idx);
            qOptions.appendChild(el);
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('.quiz-step').forEach((s, i) => {
            s.className = 'quiz-step' + (i < currentQIndex ? ' completed' : i === currentQIndex ? ' active' : '');
        });
    }

    function handleAnswer(selectedIndex) {
        if (qExplanation.classList.contains('show')) return;
        const q = questions[currentQIndex];
        const opts = qOptions.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');
        opts.forEach(o => o.classList.add('disabled'));
        opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('selected');
        if (selectedIndex === q.correct) {
            opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('correct');
            score++;
            topicScores[q.topic].correct++;
        } else {
            opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('wrong');
            opts[q.correct].classList.add('correct');
        }
        qExpText.innerText = q.explanation;
        qExplanation.classList.add('show');
    }

    btnNext.onclick = () => {
        currentQIndex++;
        if (currentQIndex < questions.length) loadQuestion();
        else showResults();
    };

    function showResults() {
        document.getElementById('postQuizSection').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('resultSection').style.display = 'block';

        const percent = Math.round((score / questions.length) * 100);

        // Save to localStorage
        const userIdx = users.findIndex(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
        if (userIdx !== -1) {
            if (!users[userIdx].progress) users[userIdx].progress = {};
            users[userIdx].progress.postTest = { score: percent, completedAt: new Date().toISOString(), topicScores };
            localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldUsers', JSON.stringify(users));
        }

        // Overall comparison
        document.getElementById('resPost').textContent = percent + '%';
        const preScore = preTestScore ?? null;
        if (preScore !== null) {
            document.getElementById('resPre').textContent = preScore + '%';
            const delta = percent - preScore;
            const deltaEl = document.getElementById('resDelta');
            deltaEl.textContent = (delta >= 0 ? '+' : '') + delta + '%';
            deltaEl.style.color = delta >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
            document.querySelector('.arrow-box i').style.color = delta >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
        } else {
            document.getElementById('resPre').textContent = 'N/A';
            document.getElementById('resDelta').textContent = '—';
        }

        // Final ring
        const ring = document.getElementById('finalRing');
        ring.textContent = percent + '%';
        ring.className = percent >= 70 ? 'score-ring pass' : 'score-ring fail';
        document.getElementById('finalTitle').textContent = percent >= 70 ? '🎉 Excellent Post-Assessment Score!' : 'Post-Assessment Complete';
        document.getElementById('finalMsg').textContent = percent >= 70
            ? 'Outstanding improvement! Your training has significantly boosted your cybersecurity knowledge.'
            : 'Thank you for completing the assessment. Review the modules and consider retaking for a better score.';

        // Topic breakdown comparison
        const topicContainer = document.getElementById('topicComparison');
        const topicColors = { 'Phishing': '#2563EB', 'Password Security': '#16A34A', 'Malware': '#DC2626', 'Safe Internet': '#0891B2', 'Social Engineering': '#7C3AED', 'Data Protection': '#D97706' };
        for (const topic in topicScores) {
            const ts = topicScores[topic];
            const postPct = Math.round((ts.correct / ts.total) * 100);
            const prePct = preTopicScores[topic] ? Math.round((preTopicScores[topic].correct / preTopicScores[topic].total) * 100) : null;
            const color = topicColors[topic] || 'var(--blue-mid)';
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'tc-row';
            row.innerHTML = `
                <div class="tc-label">${topic}</div>
                <div class="tc-bars">
                    ${prePct !== null ? `<div class="tc-bar-row"><span class="tc-bar-label pre">PRE</span><div class="tc-bar-bg"><div class="tc-bar-fill pre-bar" style="width:${prePct}%;"></div></div><span class="tc-score" style="color:var(--text-muted);">${prePct}%</span></div>` : ''}
                    <div class="tc-bar-row"><span class="tc-bar-label post">POST</span><div class="tc-bar-bg"><div class="tc-bar-fill post-bar" style="width:0%;transition:width 1.2s;" data-width="${postPct}"></div></div><span class="tc-score" style="color:var(--green);">${postPct}%</span></div>
                </div>`;
            topicContainer.appendChild(row);
        }
        // Animate bars
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-width]').forEach(bar => {
                bar.style.width = bar.dataset.width + '%';
            });
        }, 100);

        CyberSentry.celebrate();
    }

    loadQuestion();
    CyberSentry.init('module');
</script>
</body>
</html>

=== FILE: pre-assessment.html ===

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">

    <script>
        const questions = [
            // Phishing (Q1-4)
            { topic: 'Phishing', text: "What is the primary goal of a phishing attack?", options: ["Speed up computer performance", "Trick users into revealing sensitive information", "Install legitimate software updates", "Block unwanted spam"], correct: 1, explanation: "Phishing uses deception to manipulate users into providing passwords, financial information, or other sensitive data." },
            { topic: 'Phishing', text: "Which of the following is a red flag indicating a phishing email?", options: ["It is addressed to you by name from a known colleague", "It comes from an official .gov.lk address", "It creates extreme urgency ('Your account will be deleted in 1 hour!')", "It contains no links or attachments"], correct: 2, explanation: "Artificial urgency is a classic phishing tactic designed to bypass rational thinking. Legitimate government systems do not send such ultimatums via email." },
            { topic: 'Phishing', text: "What is 'Smishing'?", options: ["Phishing via phone calls", "Phishing via SMS text messages", "A type of computer virus", "A social media scam"], correct: 1, explanation: "Smishing uses SMS messages to deliver phishing attacks, often mimicking banks, government agencies, or delivery services." },
            { topic: 'Phishing', scenario: "You receive an email from 'accounts@gampaha-secretariat.org' (note: not .gov.lk) asking you to verify your login. What should you do?", text: "What is the MOST appropriate response?", options: ["Verify by clicking the link", "Email the sender to confirm", "Do not click any links; report to IT using the official IT contact", "Forward to colleagues to warn them from the suspicious computer"], correct: 2, explanation: "The domain 'gampaha-secretariat.org' is not the official '.gov.lk' domain and is likely a phishing attempt. Never click links in suspicious emails." },
            // Passwords (Q5-8)
            { topic: 'Password Security', text: "Which of the following is considered the strongest password?", options: ["pass123", "Admin@2024", "BlueSkyMango!River42", "Gampaha#1"], correct: 2, explanation: "Length is the most critical factor. A long passphrase with multiple unrelated words is exponentially harder to crack than a short complex password." },
            { topic: 'Password Security', text: "What does Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) provide?", options: ["Faster login speeds", "An extra layer of security requiring a second form of verification beyond just a password", "Automatic password generation", "Encrypted file storage"], correct: 1, explanation: "MFA requires both something you know (password) and something you have (phone/token) or something you are (biometric), making account compromise much harder." },
            { topic: 'Password Security', text: "Why should you NOT reuse your personal email password for government e-office systems?", options: ["Because personal emails have different character limits", "If your personal account is compromised, attackers can use the same credentials to access government systems", "Because government passwords must be shorter", "There is no reason — reusing strong passwords is acceptable"], correct: 1, explanation: "Credential stuffing attacks use stolen passwords from one breach to try logging into other services. Different systems must have unique passwords." },
            { topic: 'Password Security', text: "A colleague forgot their government login password and asks to use your account temporarily. What should you do?", options: ["Share it since it's a trusted colleague", "Share it but change it the next day", "Refuse and advise them to contact IT for a password reset", "Write your password on a sticky note for them"], correct: 2, explanation: "Sharing login credentials violates government IT policy and could make you liable for any actions taken under your account." },
            // Malware (Q9-12)
            { topic: 'Malware', text: "What type of malware encrypts a victim's files and demands payment for the decryption key?", options: ["Spyware", "Adware", "Ransomware", "Worm"], correct: 2, explanation: "Ransomware has become one of the most damaging threats to government institutions. Regular offline backups are the most effective defense." },
            { topic: 'Malware', text: "You find a USB drive labeled 'Ministry Budget Files' in the car park. What should you do?", options: ["Plug it in to see if it belongs to a colleague", "Plug it in but run a virus scan first", "Hand it to IT without plugging it in anywhere", "Put it in the lost and found box"], correct: 2, explanation: "This is a Baiting attack. Infected USB drives are left intentionally to compromise systems. Only the IT team with proper tools should handle unknown drives." },
            { topic: 'Malware', text: "Which action BEST prevents malware infections on government workstations?", options: ["Restarting your computer daily", "Keeping the operating system and software fully updated with security patches", "Changing your wallpaper regularly", "Using a personal email for work files"], correct: 1, explanation: "Software patches fix security vulnerabilities that malware actively exploits. Unpatched systems are major targets for attackers." },
            { topic: 'Malware', text: "If you suspect your workstation has malware, what is the FIRST action you should take?", options: ["Try to delete the suspected files yourself", "Disconnect from the network and contact IT immediately", "Continue working and see if it resolves itself", "Email your supervisor from the infected machine"], correct: 1, explanation: "Isolating an infected machine prevents the malware from spreading to other computers on the government network, limiting damage significantly." },
            // Safe Internet (Q13-15)
            { topic: 'Safe Internet', text: "What does the padlock icon (🔒) and 'https://' in a website address indicate?", options: ["The website is fast", "The connection between your browser and the website is encrypted", "The website is government-owned", "The website has been verified as safe by an antivirus program"], correct: 1, explanation: "HTTPS encrypts all data transmitted between you and the website, preventing eavesdroppers from reading your data in transit." },
            { topic: 'Safe Internet', text: "What is the main danger of using public Wi-Fi (in a café or airport) for government work?", options: ["The Wi-Fi speed is too slow for government portals", "Attackers can intercept (eavesdrop on) unencrypted data sent over the network", "Public Wi-Fi doesn't work on government laptops", "It costs more data than mobile data"], correct: 1, explanation: "Public Wi-Fi is unencrypted. A Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) attack allows an attacker on the same network to capture your login credentials and sensitive data." },
            { topic: 'Safe Internet', text: "After using a government portal on a shared office computer, what MUST you do?", options: ["Just close the browser window", "Properly log out of the portal and clear the browser session/cookies", "Turn off the monitor", "Nothing — the session expires automatically in a few days"], correct: 1, explanation: "Closing the browser doesn't always end the session. Another user could press 'back' and access your account. Always log out explicitly and clear cookies from shared computers." },
            // Social Engineering (Q16-18)
            { topic: 'Social Engineering', text: "Someone claiming to be an IT auditor calls and says they need your system password to conduct a compliance audit. This is an example of:", options: ["A legitimate audit procedure", "A Pretexting social engineering attack", "A Malware infection", "A technical support request"], correct: 1, explanation: "Pretexting involves fabricating a scenario (like an IT audit) to justify an unusual request. Legitimate IT auditors and support teams never need your password." },
            { topic: 'Social Engineering', text: "An unknown person in professional attire follows you through a security door into a restricted area. This is called:", options: ["Vishing", "Spear phishing", "Tailgating/Piggybacking", "Shoulder surfing"], correct: 2, explanation: "Tailgating bypasses physical access controls by piggybacking on an authorized person's entry. Every person entering restricted areas must use their own credentials." },
            { topic: 'Social Engineering', text: "Which psychological tactic do social engineers MOST frequently use to pressure victims?", options: ["Offering compliments and praise", "Creating urgency and invoking authority figures to prevent rational thinking", "Sending large file attachments", "Using technical jargon to confuse victims"], correct: 1, explanation: "Creating urgency (act now or face consequences) combined with authority (I'm from management/IT/police) bypasses rational decision-making and is the hallmark of social engineering." },
            // Data Protection (Q19-20)
            { topic: 'Data Protection', text: "Under Sri Lanka's PDPA 2022, what does 'Data Minimisation' require government employees to do?", options: ["Minimise the physical size of data files", "Only collect and process personal data that is strictly necessary for the stated purpose", "Delete all personal data every month", "Share data only via minimised email attachments"], correct: 1, explanation: "Data Minimisation is a fundamental PDPA principle: collect only what you need, for the specific purpose you need it for — nothing more." },
            { topic: 'Data Protection', text: "A confidential citizen welfare document is no longer needed. How should it be disposed of?", options: ["Put in the general recycling bin", "Tear in half and discard", "Shred using a cross-cut shredder and dispose of securely", "Store in a drawer indefinitely"], correct: 2, explanation: "Confidential documents must be shredded (cross-cut/confetti shredding) to ensure the information cannot be reconstructed from discarded material." },
        ];

        let currentQIndex = 0;
        let score = 0;
        const topicScores = {};

        const quizProgress = document.getElementById('quizProgress');
        const qCurrent = document.getElementById('qCurrent');
        const qScenario = document.getElementById('qScenario');
        const qText = document.getElementById('qText');
        const qOptions = document.getElementById('qOptions');
        const qExplanation = document.getElementById('qExplanation');
        const qExpText = document.getElementById('qExpText');
        const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnNextQuestion');

        // Build topic scores tracker
        questions.forEach(q => {
            if (!topicScores[q.topic]) topicScores[q.topic] = { correct: 0, total: 0 };
            topicScores[q.topic].total++;
        });

        // Init progress bar
        for (let i = 0; i < questions.length; i++) {
            const step = document.createElement('div');
            step.className = 'quiz-step';
            if (i === 0) step.classList.add('active');
            quizProgress.appendChild(step);
        }

        function loadQuestion() {
            const q = questions[currentQIndex];
            qCurrent.innerText = currentQIndex + 1;
            if (q.scenario) { qScenario.innerText = q.scenario; qScenario.style.display = "block"; }
            else { qScenario.style.display = "none"; }
            qText.innerText = q.text;
            qOptions.innerHTML = "";
            qExplanation.classList.remove('show');
            const letters = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'];
            q.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
                const optEl = document.createElement('div');
                optEl.className = 'quiz-option';
                optEl.innerHTML = `<div class="quiz-option-letter">${letters[idx]}</div><div class="quiz-option-text">${opt}</div>`;
                optEl.onclick = () => handleAnswer(idx);
                qOptions.appendChild(optEl);
            });
            const steps = document.querySelectorAll('.quiz-step');
            steps.forEach((s, i) => {
                s.classList.remove('active');
                if (i < currentQIndex) s.classList.add('completed');
                if (i === currentQIndex) s.classList.add('active');
            });
        }

        function handleAnswer(selectedIndex) {
            if (qExplanation.classList.contains('show')) return;
            const q = questions[currentQIndex];
            const opts = qOptions.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');
            opts.forEach(o => o.classList.add('disabled'));
            opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('selected');
            if (selectedIndex === q.correct) {
                opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('correct');
                score++;
                topicScores[q.topic].correct++;
            } else {
                opts[selectedIndex].classList.add('wrong');
                opts[q.correct].classList.add('correct');
            }
            qExpText.innerText = q.explanation;
            qExplanation.classList.add('show');
        }

        btnNext.onclick = () => {
            currentQIndex++;
            if (currentQIndex < questions.length) { loadQuestion(); }
            else { showResults(); }
        };

        function showResults() {
            document.getElementById('assessmentQuiz').style.display = 'none';
            const resultDiv = document.getElementById('assessmentResult');
            resultDiv.style.display = 'block';

            const percent = Math.round((score / questions.length) * 100);
            document.getElementById('resultRing').innerText = percent + '%';
            document.getElementById('resultRing').className = percent >= 70 ? 'score-ring pass' : 'score-ring fail';
            document.getElementById('resultTitle').innerText = percent >= 70 ? 'Strong Baseline Score!' : 'Pre-Assessment Complete!';
            document.getElementById('statScore').innerText = percent + '%';
            document.getElementById('statCorrect').innerText = score;
            document.getElementById('statWrong').innerText = questions.length - score;

            // Topic breakdown
            const topicBreakdown = document.getElementById('topicBreakdown');
            const colors = { 'Phishing': '#2563EB', 'Password Security': '#16A34A', 'Malware': '#DC2626', 'Safe Internet': '#0891B2', 'Social Engineering': '#7C3AED', 'Data Protection': '#D97706' };
            for (const topic in topicScores) {
                const ts = topicScores[topic];
                const pct = Math.round((ts.correct / ts.total) * 100);
                const color = colors[topic] || 'var(--blue-mid)';
                const row = document.createElement('div');
                row.className = 'topic-row';
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="topic-name">${topic}</div>
                    <div class="topic-bar-bg">
                        <div class="topic-bar-fill" style="width: ${pct}%; background: ${color};"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="topic-score" style="color: ${color};">${ts.correct}/${ts.total}</div>
                `;
                topicBreakdown.appendChild(row);
            }

            // Save to localStorage
            const currentUserId = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
            if (currentUserId && currentUserId !== 'admin') {
                let users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
                let userIndex = users.findIndex(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
                if (userIndex !== -1) {
                    if (!users[userIndex].progress) users[userIndex].progress = {};
                    users[userIndex].progress.preTest = { score: percent, completedAt: new Date().toISOString(), topicScores: topicScores };
                    localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldUsers', JSON.stringify(users));
                }
            }
        }

        loadQuestion();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

=== FILE: script.js ===

// Common logic for CyberShield SL

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    // 1. Sidebar Navigation Logic
    const sidebarItems = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item');
    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('.content-section');

    sidebarItems.forEach(item => {
        item.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const targetId = item.getAttribute('data-target');
            
            if (targetId) {
                // Update active state on sidebar
                sidebarItems.forEach(nav => nav.classList.remove('active'));
                item.classList.add('active');

                // Update visible section
                sections.forEach(sec => sec.classList.remove('active'));
                document.getElementById(targetId).classList.add('active');

                // Update Progress Bar
                const globalProgress = document.getElementById('globalProgress');
                const progressText = document.querySelector('.progress-pill span');
                
                let percent = 0;
                if (targetId === 'section1') percent = 25;
                else if (targetId === 'section2') percent = 50;
                else if (targetId === 'section3') percent = 75;
                else if (targetId === 'section4') percent = 90;

                if (globalProgress && progressText) {
                    const currentWidth = parseInt(globalProgress.style.width) || 0;
                    if (percent > currentWidth && currentWidth < 100) {
                        globalProgress.style.width = percent + '%';
                        progressText.innerText = 'Progress: ' + percent + '%';
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    });

    // 2. Simulated Video Player Logic
    const playBtn = document.querySelector('.play-button');
    const videoProgress = document.querySelector('.video-progress-fill');
    const videoTime = document.querySelector('.video-time');
    const chapterCards = document.querySelectorAll('.chapter-card');

    let isPlaying = false;
    let progress = 0;
    let videoInterval;

    if (playBtn) {
        playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            isPlaying = !isPlaying;
            if (isPlaying) {
                playBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="ti ti-player-pause-filled"></i>';
                videoInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    progress += 1;
                    if (progress > 100) progress = 0;
                    videoProgress.style.width = progress + '%';
                    
                    // Simulate time (assuming 10 min video = 600s, 1% = 6s)
                    const totalSeconds = Math.floor((progress / 100) * 600);
                    const mins = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
                    const secs = totalSeconds % 60;
                    videoTime.textContent = `${mins}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')} / 10:00`;

                    // Highlight chapters based on progress
                    chapterCards.forEach((card, index) => {
                        const chapterStart = index * 25; // simple 4 equal parts
                        if (progress >= chapterStart && progress < chapterStart + 25) {
                            chapterCards.forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                            card.classList.add('active');
                        }
                    });
                }, 500); // Fast simulation for demo
            } else {
                playBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="ti ti-player-play-filled"></i>';
                clearInterval(videoInterval);
            }
        });
    }

    // Interactive exercises will be handled within their respective HTML files
    // due to unique logic per module, but we can set up global utility functions here.
});

// Utility to get current user
function getCurrentUser() {
    return localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
}

// Utility to get all users
function getAllUsers() {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
}

// Utility to save progress
function saveModuleProgress(moduleNum, score, passed) {
    const currentUserId = getCurrentUser();
    if (!currentUserId || currentUserId === 'admin') return;

    let users = getAllUsers();
    let userIndex = users.findIndex(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
    
    if (userIndex !== -1) {
        if (!users[userIndex].progress) {
            users[userIndex].progress = {};
        }
        users[userIndex].progress[`module${moduleNum}`] = {
            score: score,
            passed: passed,
            completedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        };
        localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldUsers', JSON.stringify(users));
    }

    // Trigger Sentry mascot reaction
    if (typeof CyberSentry !== 'undefined') {
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (passed) {
                CyberSentry.celebrate();
            } else {
                CyberSentry.showFailTip();
            }
        }, 500);
    }
}


// Utility to get progress
function getModuleProgress() {
    const currentUserId = getCurrentUser();
    if (!currentUserId || currentUserId === 'admin') return {};

    let users = getAllUsers();
    let user = users.find(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
    
    return (user && user.progress) ? user.progress : {};
}

=== FILE: sentry.js ===

/**
 * CyberShield SL — Cyber Sentry 3D AI Robot Assistant Mascot
 * Floating animated 3D robot character with interactive website tour.
 */

const CyberSentry = (() => {

  const SVG_SENTRY = `

  const TIPS = {

  const TOUR_STEPS = [

  let tipIndex = 0;
  let tourIndex = 0;
  let inTourMode = false;
  let currentContext = 'login';
  let isVisible = false;

  function detectContext() {
    const path = window.location.pathname.toLowerCase();
    if (path.includes('module')) return 'module';
    if (path.includes('dashboard')) return 'dashboard';
    return 'login';
  }

  function injectDOM() {
    if (document.getElementById('cyberSentryWrapper')) return;

    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className = 'sentry-wrapper';
    wrapper.id = 'cyberSentryWrapper';

    wrapper.innerHTML = `
      <div class="sentry-bubble" id="sentryBubble">
        <button class="bubble-close" onclick="CyberSentry.hideBubble()" title="Close">×</button>

        ${SVG_SENTRY}
      </div>
    `;

    document.body.appendChild(wrapper);
  }

  function showTip(context, tipSet) {
    if (inTourMode) return;
    currentContext = context || detectContext();
    const tips = tipSet || TIPS[currentContext] || TIPS['login'];
    tipIndex = 0;
    renderTip(tips[tipIndex]);
    document.getElementById('sentryBubble').classList.add('show');
    isVisible = true;
  }

  function renderTip(tip) {
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('sentryBubbleTitle');
    const textEl = document.getElementById('sentryBubbleText');
    if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = tip.title;
    if (textEl) textEl.textContent = tip.text;
  }

  function nextTip() {
    if (inTourMode) {
      nextTourStep();
      return;
    }
    const tips = TIPS[currentContext] || TIPS['login'];
    tipIndex = (tipIndex + 1) % tips.length;
    renderTip(tips[tipIndex]);
  }

  function hideBubble() {
    if (inTourMode) endTour();
    const bubble = document.getElementById('sentryBubble');
    if (bubble) bubble.classList.remove('show');
    isVisible = false;
  }

  function toggleBubble() {
    if (isVisible) {
      hideBubble();
    } else {
      showTip(currentContext);
    }
  }

  // ── GUIDED WEBSITE TOUR ENGINE ──────────────────────────────────────
  function startTour() {
    inTourMode = true;
    tourIndex = 0;
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('cyberSentryWrapper');
    if (wrapper) wrapper.classList.add('tour-mode');
    showTourStep(tourIndex);
  }

  function showTourStep(index) {
    if (index < 0 || index >= TOUR_STEPS.length) {
      endTour();
      return;
    }

    const step = TOUR_STEPS[index];
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('cyberSentryWrapper');
    const controls = document.getElementById('sentryControls');

    // Remove previous highlights
    document.querySelectorAll('.tour-highlight-box').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('tour-highlight-box'));

    // Highlight target element if present
    const targetEl = document.querySelector(step.target);
    if (targetEl) {
      targetEl.classList.add('tour-highlight-box');
      targetEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    }

    // Position assistant
    if (wrapper && step.pos) {
      wrapper.style.top = step.pos.top;
      wrapper.style.bottom = step.pos.bottom;
      wrapper.style.right = step.pos.right;
      wrapper.style.left = step.pos.left;
    }

    renderTip({ title: step.title, text: step.text });

    if (controls) {
      controls.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; width:100%; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
          <button onclick="CyberSentry.prevTourStep()" class="sentry-btn-tour" ${index === 0 ? 'disabled style="opacity:0.5"' : ''}>← Prev</button>
          <span style="font-size:11px; color:#94a3b8; font-weight:700;">${index + 1} / ${TOUR_STEPS.length}</span>
          <button onclick="CyberSentry.nextTourStep()" class="sentry-btn-primary">${index === TOUR_STEPS.length - 1 ? 'Finish 🎉' : 'Next →'}</button>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    document.getElementById('sentryBubble')?.classList.add('show');
    isVisible = true;
  }

  function nextTourStep() {
    tourIndex++;
    if (tourIndex >= TOUR_STEPS.length) {
      endTour();
    } else {
      showTourStep(tourIndex);
    }
  }

  function prevTourStep() {
    if (tourIndex > 0) {
      tourIndex--;
      showTourStep(tourIndex);
    }
  }

  function endTour() {
    inTourMode = false;
    document.querySelectorAll('.tour-highlight-box').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('tour-highlight-box'));

    const wrapper = document.getElementById('cyberSentryWrapper');
    if (wrapper) {
      wrapper.classList.remove('tour-mode');
      wrapper.style.top = 'auto';
      wrapper.style.bottom = '24px';
      wrapper.style.right = '28px';
      wrapper.style.left = 'auto';
    }

    const controls = document.getElementById('sentryControls');
    if (controls) {
      controls.innerHTML = `
        <button onclick="CyberSentry.nextTip()" class="sentry-btn-primary">Next Tip →</button>
        <button onclick="CyberSentry.startTour()" class="sentry-btn-tour">Website Tour 🚀</button>
      `;
    }

    renderTip({ title: "Tour Completed! 🎉", text: "You're all set! Explore the modules and let me know if you need any tips." });
  }

  function celebrate() {
    const avatar = document.getElementById('sentryAvatar');
    if (!avatar) return;
    avatar.classList.add('celebrating');
    showTip(null, TIPS.quiz_pass);
    launchConfetti();
    setTimeout(() => avatar.classList.remove('celebrating'), 4000);
  }

  function showFailTip() {
    showTip(null, TIPS.quiz_fail);
  }

  function launchConfetti() {
    const container = document.createElement('div');
    container.className = 'confetti-container';
    document.body.appendChild(container);
    const colors = ['#F59E0B', '#3B82F6', '#10B981', '#EF4444', '#8B5CF6', '#EC4899', '#ffffff'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
      const piece = document.createElement('div');
      piece.className = 'confetti-piece';
      piece.style.cssText = `
        left: ${Math.random() * 100}%;
        width: ${6 + Math.random() * 8}px;
        height: ${6 + Math.random() * 8}px;
        background: ${colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)]};
        animation-duration: ${1.5 + Math.random() * 2}s;
        animation-delay: ${Math.random() * 0.8}s;
        border-radius: ${Math.random() > 0.5 ? '50%' : '2px'};
      `;
      container.appendChild(piece);
    }
    setTimeout(() => container.remove(), 4000);
  }

  function init(context) {
    injectDOM();
    currentContext = context || detectContext();
    setTimeout(() => showTip(currentContext), 1200);
  }

  return { init, showTip, hideBubble, toggleBubble, nextTip, startTour, nextTourStep, prevTourStep, endTour, celebrate, showFailTip, launchConfetti };
})();

=== FILE: server.py ===
