"""
server.py — Mock SIEM Telemetry Pipeline  (Level 3 — Real-time Dashboard)
==========================================================================
Adds:
  • In-memory ring-buffer for last 500 events  (collections.deque)
  • Server-Sent Events stream  GET /api/stream
  • Pre-computed stats endpoint  GET /api/stats
  • Root route  GET /  → serves dashboard.html
  • Thread-safe SSE client registry

Author  : Backend Security Engineer
Version : 2.0.0
"""

import json
import logging
import queue
import sys
import threading
import uuid
from collections import deque, defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from functools import wraps

from flask import Flask, Response, request, jsonify, send_from_directory
from flask_cors import CORS

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# CONFIGURATION
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

LOG_FILE        = "siem_security_events.log"
API_KEY_HEADER  = "X-API-Key"
VALID_API_KEY   = "siem-dev-secret-2026"   # Replace with env-var in production
EVENT_BUFFER    = 500                       # Max events kept in memory
TIMELINE_MINS   = 10                        # Rolling window for events/min chart

SEVERITY_COLORS = {
    "CRITICAL": "\033[1;31m",
    "HIGH"    : "\033[0;31m",
    "MEDIUM"  : "\033[0;33m",
    "LOW"     : "\033[0;32m",
    "INFO"    : "\033[0;36m",
}
RESET = "\033[0m"

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# IN-MEMORY STATE
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

event_store: deque = deque(maxlen=EVENT_BUFFER)   # ring buffer of enriched events
sse_clients: list  = []                            # list of queue.Queue, one per SSE client
state_lock         = threading.Lock()             # protects both event_store and sse_clients

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# LOGGING SETUP
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class JsonFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    def format(self, record):
        timestamp = datetime.fromtimestamp(record.created, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat()
        return f"{timestamp} - {record.levelname} - {record.getMessage()}"


def configure_logger():
    # Force UTF-8 on Windows console so unicode chars render correctly
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    logger = logging.getLogger("siem")
    logger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

    fh = logging.FileHandler(LOG_FILE, encoding="utf-8")
    fh.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    fh.setFormatter(JsonFormatter())

    sh = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    sh.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
    sh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s",
                                      datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"))
    logger.addHandler(fh)
    logger.addHandler(sh)
    return logger


logger = configure_logger()

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# FLASK APPLICATION
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

app = Flask(__name__, static_folder=".", static_url_path="")
CORS(app, resources={r"/api/*": {"origins": "*"}})

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# MIDDLEWARE
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def require_api_key(f):
    @wraps(f)
    def decorated(*args, **kwargs):
        key = request.headers.get(API_KEY_HEADER)
        if key != VALID_API_KEY:
            logger.warning("Rejected — bad API key from %s", request.remote_addr)
            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Unauthorized.", "code": 401}), 401
        return f(*args, **kwargs)
    return decorated

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# HELPERS
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

REQUIRED_FIELDS    = {"source", "event_type", "severity"}
ALLOWED_SEVERITIES = {"CRITICAL", "HIGH", "MEDIUM", "LOW", "INFO"}


def validate_payload(data):
    missing = REQUIRED_FIELDS - data.keys()
    if missing:
        return False, f"Missing required fields: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}"
    sev = str(data.get("severity", "")).upper()
    if sev not in ALLOWED_SEVERITIES:
        return False, f"Invalid severity '{sev}'. Allowed: {', '.join(sorted(ALLOWED_SEVERITIES))}"
    return True, ""


def enrich_event(data):
    return {
        "event_id"   : str(uuid.uuid4()),
        "ingested_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "source_ip"  : request.remote_addr,
        "user_agent" : request.headers.get("User-Agent", "unknown"),
        **data,
        "severity"   : data["severity"].upper(),
    }


def print_siem_alert(event):
    sev   = event.get("severity", "INFO")
    color = SEVERITY_COLORS.get(sev, "")
    border = "=" * 62
    print(f"\n{color}+{border}+")
    print(f"|  🚨 [SIEM ALERT]   Severity: {sev:<10}                      |")
    print(f"+{border}+")
    print(f"|  Event ID   : {event.get('event_id', 'N/A')}")
    print(f"|  Source     : {event.get('source', 'N/A')}")
    print(f"|  Event Type : {event.get('event_type', 'N/A')}")
    print(f"|  Source IP  : {event.get('source_ip', 'N/A')}")
    print(f"|  Ingested   : {event.get('ingested_at', 'N/A')}")
    if "message" in event:
        print(f"|  Message    : {event['message']}")
    print(f"+{border}+{RESET}\n")


def broadcast_sse(event):
    """Push a JSON event to every connected SSE client queue."""
    payload = json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False)
    dead = []
    with state_lock:
        for q in sse_clients:
            try:
                q.put_nowait(payload)
            except queue.Full:
                dead.append(q)
        for q in dead:
            sse_clients.remove(q)


def compute_stats():
    """Compute dashboard stats from the in-memory event store."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    severity_counts = defaultdict(int)
    ip_counts       = defaultdict(int)
    # Build timeline buckets: one entry per minute for last TIMELINE_MINS minutes
    timeline = {}
    for i in range(TIMELINE_MINS):
        bucket = (now - timedelta(minutes=TIMELINE_MINS - 1 - i)).strftime("%H:%M")
        timeline[bucket] = 0

    with state_lock:
        events_snapshot = list(event_store)

    for ev in events_snapshot:
        sev = ev.get("severity", "INFO")
        severity_counts[sev] += 1
        ip = ev.get("source_ip", "unknown")
        ip_counts[ip] += 1
        try:
            ts = datetime.fromisoformat(ev["ingested_at"])
            bucket = ts.strftime("%H:%M")
            if bucket in timeline:
                timeline[bucket] += 1
        except (KeyError, ValueError):
            pass

    top_ips = sorted(ip_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5]

    return {
        "total_events"    : len(events_snapshot),
        "severity_counts" : dict(severity_counts),
        "timeline"        : timeline,
        "top_ips"         : [{"ip": ip, "count": cnt} for ip, cnt in top_ips],
        "events_per_min"  : round(len(events_snapshot) / max(TIMELINE_MINS, 1), 2),
        "active_sources"  : len({ev.get("source") for ev in events_snapshot if ev.get("source")}),
    }

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# ROUTES
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.route("/")
def serve_dashboard():
    """Serve the real-time dashboard."""
    return send_from_directory(".", "dashboard.html")


@app.route("/api/telemetry", methods=["POST"])
@require_api_key
def ingest_telemetry():
    """
    POST /api/telemetry
    Validates → enriches → stores in-memory → logs to file → broadcasts SSE.
    """
    if not request.is_json:
        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Content-Type must be application/json.", "code": 415}), 415

    try:
        raw_data = request.get_json(force=False, silent=False)
    except Exception:
        logger.error("Malformed JSON from %s", request.remote_addr)
        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Malformed JSON payload.", "code": 400}), 400

    if not isinstance(raw_data, dict):
        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Payload must be a JSON object.", "code": 400}), 400

    is_valid, error_msg = validate_payload(raw_data)
    if not is_valid:
        return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": error_msg, "code": 422}), 422

    event = enrich_event(raw_data)

    # Store in ring buffer
    with state_lock:
        event_store.append(event)

    # Console alert
    print_siem_alert(event)

    # Persist to log file
    log_level = (
        logging.CRITICAL if event["severity"] == "CRITICAL" else
        logging.ERROR    if event["severity"] == "HIGH"     else
        logging.WARNING  if event["severity"] == "MEDIUM"   else
        logging.INFO
    )
    logger.log(log_level, json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False))

    # Push to all SSE clients
    broadcast_sse(event)

    return jsonify({
        "status"  : "ok",
        "message" : "Telemetry event ingested successfully.",
        "event_id": event["event_id"],
        "code"    : 200
    }), 200


@app.route("/api/stream")
def sse_stream():
    """
    GET /api/stream
    Server-Sent Events endpoint — each connected browser tab gets a
    dedicated Queue. New events are pushed within milliseconds of ingestion.
    No authentication on purpose: SSE is read-only and carries no secrets.
    """
    client_queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=100)

    with state_lock:
        sse_clients.append(client_queue)

    logger.debug("SSE client connected. Total clients: %d", len(sse_clients))

    def event_generator():
        # Send a heartbeat comment every 15 s to keep the connection alive
        # and an initial "connected" event so the browser knows it's live.
        try:
            yield ": connected\n\n"
            while True:
                try:
                    payload = client_queue.get(timeout=15)
                    yield f"data: {payload}\n\n"
                except queue.Empty:
                    yield ": heartbeat\n\n"   # SSE comment — keeps connection alive
        except GeneratorExit:
            pass
        finally:
            with state_lock:
                try:
                    sse_clients.remove(client_queue)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
            logger.debug("SSE client disconnected. Total clients: %d", len(sse_clients))

    return Response(
        event_generator(),
        mimetype="text/event-stream",
        headers={
            "Cache-Control"      : "no-cache",
            "X-Accel-Buffering"  : "no",     # Disable Nginx proxy buffering
            "Connection"         : "keep-alive",
        }
    )


@app.route("/api/stats")
@require_api_key
def get_stats():
    """GET /api/stats — Pre-computed dashboard metrics from the event ring-buffer."""
    return jsonify({"status": "ok", **compute_stats()}), 200


@app.route("/api/events")
@require_api_key
def list_recent_events():
    """GET /api/events?limit=50 — Returns last N events from the ring-buffer."""
    limit = max(1, min(request.args.get("limit", 50, type=int), 500))
    with state_lock:
        snapshot = list(event_store)[-limit:]
    return jsonify({"status": "ok", "total": len(snapshot), "events": snapshot}), 200


@app.route("/api/health")
def health_check():
    return jsonify({
        "status"        : "healthy",
        "timestamp"     : datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "service"       : "mock-siem-telemetry-pipeline",
        "version"       : "2.0.0",
        "sse_clients"   : len(sse_clients),
        "events_buffered": len(event_store),
    }), 200

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# ERROR HANDLERS
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.errorhandler(404)
def not_found(e):
    return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Endpoint not found.", "code": 404}), 404

@app.errorhandler(405)
def method_not_allowed(e):
    return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Method not allowed.", "code": 405}), 405

@app.errorhandler(500)
def internal_error(e):
    logger.critical("Unhandled server error: %s", str(e))
    return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "Internal server error.", "code": 500}), 500

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# ENTRYPOINT
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    logger.info("SIEM Telemetry Pipeline v2.0 - Real-time Dashboard Edition")
    logger.info("[*] Dashboard  : http://localhost:5000")
    logger.info("[*] SSE Stream : http://localhost:5000/api/stream")
    logger.info("[*] Stats API  : http://localhost:5000/api/stats")
    logger.info("[*] Log file   : %s", LOG_FILE)
    app.run(host="0.0.0.0", port=5000, debug=True, threaded=True)

    {
        num: 1, file: 'module1.html',

// ── AUTH CHECK ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const currentId = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
if (!currentId || currentId === 'admin') {
    window.location.href = currentId === 'admin' ? 'admin.html' : 'index.html';
}

// ── LOAD USER DATA ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const users    = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
const user     = users.find(u => u.empId === currentId) || {};
const progress = user.progress || {};

// Populate header & hero
const firstName = user.firstName || user.name?.split(' ')[0] || currentId;
document.getElementById('heroName').textContent = firstName;
document.getElementById('heroDivision').textContent = user.division || '—';
document.getElementById('heroId').textContent = user.empId || currentId;
document.getElementById('topbarName').textContent = user.name || currentId;
document.getElementById('avatarInitial').textContent = (user.name || currentId).charAt(0).toUpperCase();

// ── CALCULATE STATS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let completedCount = 0, passedCount = 0, totalScore = 0, scoredModules = 0;
MODULES.forEach(m => {
    const key = `module${m.num}`;
    if (progress[key]) {
        completedCount++;
        if (progress[key].passed) passedCount++;
        if (progress[key].score != null) {
            totalScore += progress[key].score;
            scoredModules++;
        }
    }
});

const overallPct = Math.round((passedCount / MODULES.length) * 100);
document.getElementById('statCompleted').textContent = completedCount;
document.getElementById('statPassed').textContent = passedCount;
document.getElementById('statAvgScore').textContent = scoredModules > 0 ? (totalScore / scoredModules).toFixed(1) : '—';
document.getElementById('overallPct').textContent = overallPct + '%';
document.getElementById('overallBarFill').style.width = overallPct + '%';

// ── RENDER MODULES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderModules() {
    const grid = document.getElementById('modulesGrid');
    grid.innerHTML = '';

    MODULES.forEach((m) => {
        const key = `module${m.num}`;
        const prog = progress[key];
        const isDone = !!prog;
        const isPassed = prog?.passed;
        const score = prog?.score;
        const progressPct = isDone ? (isPassed ? 100 : 60) : 0;

        // Score tag HTML
        let scoreTag = '';
        if (score != null) {
            scoreTag = isPassed
                ? `<span class="module-score-tag score-pass"><i class="ti ti-circle-check"></i> ${score}/10 — Passed</span>`
                : `<span class="module-score-tag score-fail"><i class="ti ti-circle-x"></i> ${score}/10 — Retry</span>`;
        } else {
            scoreTag = `<span class="module-score-tag score-none"><i class="ti ti-circle"></i> Not started</span>`;
        }

        // Button
        let btnClass = isDone ? 'success' : 'primary';
        let btnLabel = isDone ? (isPassed ? '↩ Review' : '🔄 Retry') : '▶ Start';

        // Status Icon
        let statusIconHtml = `<i class="ti ti-circle-arrow-right-filled" style="color: var(--brand-blue);"></i>`;
        if (isDone) {
            statusIconHtml = isPassed
                ? `<i class="ti ti-circle-check-filled" style="color: var(--accent-green);"></i>`
                : `<i class="ti ti-alert-triangle-filled" style="color: var(--accent-amber);"></i>`;
        }

        const card = document.createElement('a');
        card.href = m.file;
        card.className = `module-card${isDone && isPassed ? ' completed' : ''}`;
        card.innerHTML = `
            <div class="module-card-accent-bar" style="background: ${m.accentColor};"></div>
            <div class="module-card-header">
                <span class="module-num-badge">Module 0${m.num}</span>
                <span class="module-status-icon">${statusIconHtml}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="module-icon-wrap" style="background: ${m.bgSoft}; border-color: ${m.borderSoft};">
                <img src="${m.iconImg}" alt="${m.title}" class="module-icon-img">
            </div>
            <div class="module-card-title">${m.title}</div>
            <div class="module-card-desc">${m.desc}</div>
            <div class="module-card-meta">
                <span><i class="ti ti-clock"></i> ${m.duration}</span>
                <span><i class="ti ti-chart-bar"></i> ${m.level}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="module-progress-bar">
                <div class="module-progress-fill" style="width:${progressPct}%; background: ${m.accentColor};"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="module-card-footer">
                ${scoreTag}
                <button class="btn-start ${btnClass}" onclick="event.preventDefault(); window.location.href='${m.file}'">${btnLabel}</button>
            </div>
        `;
        grid.appendChild(card);
    });
}
renderModules();

// ── SIEM MODAL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function openSiemModal(e) {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    document.getElementById('siemModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').focus();
}
function closeSiemModal() {
    document.getElementById('siemModal').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').value = '';
}
function goToSiem() {
    const key = document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').value.trim();
    if (!key) {
        document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').style.borderColor = 'var(--accent-red)';
        setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').style.borderColor = '', 1500);
        return;
    }
    sessionStorage.setItem('siem_api_key', key);
    window.location.href = 'dashboard.html';
}
document.getElementById('siemKeyInput').addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') goToSiem();
    if (e.key === 'Escape') closeSiemModal();
});
document.getElementById('siemModal').addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (e.target === document.getElementById('siemModal')) closeSiemModal();
});

// ── LOGOUT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function doLogout(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    localStorage.removeItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
    window.location.href = 'index.html';
}

// ── TOAST ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function showToast(msg, emoji = '✅') {
    const wrap = document.getElementById('toastWrap');
    const t = document.createElement('div');
    t.className = 'toast';
    t.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size:18px">${emoji}</span> ${msg}`;
    wrap.appendChild(t);
    setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 3500);
}

// Welcome toast
const lang = LangUtil.getCurrentLang();
const welcomeMsg = lang === 'si' ? `නැවත සාදරයෙන් පිළිගනිමු, ${firstName}!` : `Welcome back, ${firstName}!`;
setTimeout(() => showToast(welcomeMsg, '👋'), 600);
if (passedCount === MODULES.length) {
    const certMsg = lang === 'si' ? '🎉 පාඩම් 5ම සමත් විය! සම්මාන පත්‍රය ලබාගන්න!' : '🎉 All 5 modules completed! Get your certificate!';
    setTimeout(() => showToast(certMsg, '🏆'), 1400);
}

// Init Cyber Sentry mascot for student dashboard
if (typeof CyberSentry !== 'undefined') CyberSentry.init('dashboard');

// ── ANIMATED LIVE WALLPAPER & CANVASES ENGINE ─────────────────────────
let currentWpMode = localStorage.getItem('cyberShield_wp_mode') || 'items';
let isDarkTheme = localStorage.getItem('cyberShield_dark_theme') === 'true';
let animFrameId = null;

const bgCanvas = document.getElementById('cyberCanvas');
const bgOverlay = document.getElementById('bgOverlay');
const ctx = bgCanvas ? bgCanvas.getContext('2d') : null;

function initCanvasSize() {
    if (!bgCanvas) return;
    bgCanvas.width = window.innerWidth;
    bgCanvas.height = window.innerHeight;
}
window.addEventListener('resize', initCanvasSize);
initCanvasSize();

// Particle & Matrix system states
let particles = [];
let matrixColumns = [];
let waveOffset = 0;

function setupParticleMode() {
    particles = [];
    const count = Math.floor((bgCanvas.width * bgCanvas.height) / 15000);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        particles.push({
            x: Math.random() * bgCanvas.width,
            y: Math.random() * bgCanvas.height,
            vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
            vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
            radius: Math.random() * 2 + 1,
            color: Math.random() > 0.5 ? 'rgba(59, 130, 246, ' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, '
        });
    }
}

function setupMatrixMode() {
    matrixColumns = [];
    const fontSize = 14;
    const cols = Math.floor(bgCanvas.width / fontSize);
    for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) {
        matrixColumns[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * -50);
    }
}

function renderCanvasFrame() {
    if (!ctx || !bgCanvas) return;

    if (currentWpMode === 'video_cyber') {
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, bgCanvas.width, bgCanvas.height);
        animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(renderCanvasFrame);
        return;
    }

    if (currentWpMode === 'matrix') {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 13, 26, 0.15)';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, bgCanvas.width, bgCanvas.height);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#06b6d4';
        ctx.font = '13px monospace';
        const chars = '01CYBERSHIELD1001010101';
        const fontSize = 14;

        for (let i = 0; i < matrixColumns.length; i++) {
            const char = chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
            const x = i * fontSize;
            const y = matrixColumns[i] * fontSize;

            ctx.fillStyle = i % 5 === 0 ? '#38bdf8' : 'rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.85)';
            ctx.fillText(char, x, y);

            if (y > bgCanvas.height && Math.random() > 0.975) {
                matrixColumns[i] = 0;
            }
            matrixColumns[i]++;
        }
    } else if (currentWpMode === 'particle') {
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, bgCanvas.width, bgCanvas.height);
        
        for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
            const p1 = particles[i];
            p1.x += p1.vx;
            p1.y += p1.vy;

            if (p1.x < 0 || p1.x > bgCanvas.width) p1.vx *= -1;
            if (p1.y < 0 || p1.y > bgCanvas.height) p1.vy *= -1;

            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(p1.x, p1.y, p1.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = p1.color + '0.7)';
            ctx.fill();

            for (let j = i + 1; j < particles.length; j++) {
                const p2 = particles[j];
                const dx = p1.x - p2.x;
                const dy = p1.y - p2.y;
                const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

                if (dist < 120) {
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);
                    ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);
                    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(59, 130, 246, ${1 - dist / 120})`;
                    ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                    ctx.stroke();
                }
            }
        }
    } else if (currentWpMode === 'wave') {
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, bgCanvas.width, bgCanvas.height);
        waveOffset += 0.02;

        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.strokeStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.25)' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2)';
            
            for (let x = 0; x < bgCanvas.width; x += 15) {
                const y = bgCanvas.height * 0.5 + Math.sin(x * 0.005 + waveOffset + i) * 60 + Math.cos(x * 0.002 + waveOffset) * 40;
                if (x === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
                else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
            }
            ctx.stroke();
        }
    }

    animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(renderCanvasFrame);
}

function setWpMode(mode) {
    currentWpMode = mode;
    localStorage.setItem('cyberShield_wp_mode', mode);

    document.querySelectorAll('.wp-preset-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    const movingWrap = document.getElementById('movingItemsWrap');

    if (mode === 'items') {
        document.getElementById('wpCardItems')?.classList.add('active');
        if (movingWrap) movingWrap.style.display = 'block';
        if (bgCanvas) bgCanvas.style.opacity = '0.4';
        setupParticleMode();
    } else {
        if (movingWrap) movingWrap.style.display = 'none';
        if (bgCanvas) bgCanvas.style.opacity = '1';
        
        if (mode === 'matrix') {
            document.getElementById('wpCardMatrix')?.classList.add('active');
            setupMatrixMode();
        } else if (mode === 'particle') {
            document.getElementById('wpCardNodes')?.classList.add('active');
            setupParticleMode();
        } else if (mode === 'wave') {
            document.getElementById('wpCardWave')?.classList.add('active');
        }
    }

    if (!animFrameId) renderCanvasFrame();
}

function toggleWpModal() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('wpModalPanel');
    if (modal) modal.classList.toggle('active');
}

function toggleVideoPlay() {
    if (!bgVideo) return;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnTogglePlay');
    if (bgVideo.paused) {
        bgVideo.play();
        isVideoPlaying = true;
        if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ti ti-pause"></i> Pause Video';
    } else {
        bgVideo.pause();
        isVideoPlaying = false;
        if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ti ti-player-play"></i> Play Video';
    }
}

function toggleDarkTheme() {
    isDarkTheme = !isDarkTheme;
    localStorage.setItem('cyberShield_dark_theme', isDarkTheme);
    applyThemeMode();
}

function applyThemeMode() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnToggleTheme');
    if (isDarkTheme) {
        document.body.classList.add('dark-theme-mode');
        if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ti ti-sun"></i> Light Mode';
    } else {
        document.body.classList.remove('dark-theme-mode');
        if (btn) btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ti ti-moon"></i> Cyber Mode';
    }
}

function changeWpOpacity(val) {
    if (bgVideo) bgVideo.style.opacity = val / 100;
    const label = document.getElementById('opacityVal');
    if (label) label.textContent = val + '%';
}

function changeWpBlur(val) {
    if (bgOverlay) bgOverlay.style.backdropFilter = `blur(${val}px)`;
    const label = document.getElementById('blurVal');
    if (label) label.textContent = val + 'px';
}

// Close modal when clicking outside
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('wpModalPanel');
    const btn = document.getElementById('wpCtrlBtn');
    if (modal && modal.classList.contains('active') && !modal.contains(e.target) && !btn.contains(e.target)) {
        modal.classList.remove('active');
    }
});

// Initialize wallpaper & theme settings on load
applyThemeMode();
setWpMode(currentWpMode);
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        function submitSurvey() {
            // Validate required fields
            const required = ['q1','q2','q3','q4','q5','q6','q7','q8','q9','q10','overall_rating','q12'];
            const labels = {
                q1: 'Q1', q2: 'Q2', q3: 'Q3', q4: 'Q4', q5: 'Q5', q6: 'Q6', q7: 'Q7',
                q8: 'Q8', q9: 'Q9', q10: 'Q10 (Most Valuable Module)', overall_rating: 'Q11 (Overall Rating)', q12: 'Q12 (Recommendation)'
            };
            let missing = [];
            const responses = {};

            for (const name of required) {
                const selected = document.querySelector(`input[name="${name}"]:checked`);
                if (!selected) {
                    missing.push(labels[name]);
                } else {
                    responses[name] = selected.value;
                }
            }

            if (missing.length > 0) {
                alert(`Please answer the following required questions before submitting:\n\n${missing.join('\n')}`);
                return;
            }

            // Get optional text responses
            responses.q13 = document.getElementById('q13').value;
            responses.q14 = document.getElementById('q14').value;
            responses.q15 = document.getElementById('q15').value;

            // Save to localStorage
            const currentUserId = localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldCurrentUser');
            if (currentUserId && currentUserId !== 'admin') {
                let users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cyberShieldUsers')) || [];
                let userIndex = users.findIndex(u => u.empId === currentUserId);
                if (userIndex !== -1) {
                    if (!users[userIndex].progress) users[userIndex].progress = {};
                    users[userIndex].progress.survey = { responses, submittedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
                    localStorage.setItem('cyberShieldUsers', JSON.stringify(users));
                }
            }

            // Show thank you screen
            document.getElementById('surveyForm').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('thankYouScreen').style.display = 'block';

            // Build summary
            const likertLabels = { '1': 'Strongly Disagree', '2': 'Disagree', '3': 'Neutral', '4': 'Agree', '5': 'Strongly Agree' };
            const summaryEl = document.getElementById('surveyRatingSummary');
            const summaryItems = [
                { label: 'Overall Rating', value: '⭐'.repeat(parseInt(responses.overall_rating)) },
                { label: 'Recommend to Colleagues', value: responses.q12 },
                { label: 'Most Valuable Module', value: responses.q10 },
                { label: 'Avg. Content Quality', value: Math.round((parseInt(responses.q4) + parseInt(responses.q5) + parseInt(responses.q6) + parseInt(responses.q7)) / 4 * 20) + '% (very good)' }
            ];
            summaryItems.forEach(item => {
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.style.cssText = 'display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-size: 13px; align-items: flex-start; gap: 8px;';
                div.innerHTML = `<span style="color: var(--gray-600);">${item.label}:</span><span style="font-weight: 600; text-align: right;">${item.value}</span>`;
                summaryEl.appendChild(div);
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

=== FILE: test_events.py ===

import urllib.request, json, time

time.sleep(3)

API = "http://localhost:5000/api/telemetry"
KEY = "siem-dev-secret-2026"

events = [
    {"source": "firewall-01", "event_type": "BRUTE_FORCE",   "severity": "CRITICAL", "message": "10 failed SSH logins from 10.0.0.5"},
    {"source": "ids-sensor",  "event_type": "PORT_SCAN",     "severity": "HIGH",     "message": "Port scan detected on 80,443,22"},
    {"source": "waf-proxy",   "event_type": "SQL_INJECTION",  "severity": "HIGH",     "message": "SQLi attempt on /api/login"},
    {"source": "app-server",  "event_type": "AUTH_FAILURE",  "severity": "MEDIUM",   "message": "Bad token from 192.168.1.20"},
    {"source": "monitor-01",  "event_type": "SYSTEM_HEALTH", "severity": "INFO",     "message": "All systems nominal"},
]

for ev in events:
    body = json.dumps(ev).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        API, data=body,
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "X-API-Key": KEY},
        method="POST"
    )
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req) as r:
        resp = json.loads(r.read())
        sev = ev["severity"]
        etype = ev["event_type"]
        eid = resp["event_id"]
        print(f"[{sev:8}] {etype:20} -> {eid}")

print("All 5 test events sent successfully!")
